      <!DOCTYPE html>
      <html lang="en">
      <head>
      <meta charset="utf-8">
      <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
      <meta name="color-scheme" content="dark light">
      <title>MiniSketch - 2D constraint sketcher</title>
      <link rel="stylesheet" href="styles.css">
      <script>
        // Set the theme before first paint so a dark UI never flashes light.
        (function () {
          var t = 'dark';
          try {
            var mp = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('theme');
            if (mp === 'light' || mp === 'dark') {
              t = mp;
            } else {
              var saved = localStorage.getItem('minisketch.theme');
              if (saved === 'light' || saved === 'dark') t = saved;
            }
          } catch (err) { /* storage blocked */ }
          document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', t);
        })();
      </script>
      </head>

                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini" id="qb-fit" title="Fit view (F)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2 6V2h4M14 2h4v4M18 14v4h-4M6 18H2v-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6"/><rect x="6.5" y="6.5" width="7" height="7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-dasharray="2 1.5"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini" id="qb-selectall" title="Select all (Ctrl+A)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="3" width="10" height="10" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-dasharray="2.5 2"/><rect x="7" y="7" width="10" height="10" fill="currentColor" opacity="0.55"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini qb-act" id="qb-copy" title="Copy (Ctrl+C)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="7" y="7" width="9" height="9" rx="1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M13 7V5a1 1 0 0 0-1-1H5a1 1 0 0 0-1 1v7a1 1 0 0 0 1 1h2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini" id="qb-paste" title="Paste (Ctrl+V)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="4" y="4" width="12" height="13" rx="1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M7 4V2.5h6V4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M7.5 10h5M7.5 13h5" stroke="currentColor"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini qb-act" id="qb-dup" title="Duplicate in place (Ctrl+D)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="2.5" y="6.5" width="9" height="9" rx="1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><rect x="8.5" y="4.5" width="9" height="9" rx="1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-dasharray="2 1.6"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini qb-act" id="qb-delete" title="Delete selection (Del)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 6h10l-.8 11H5.8z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M3.5 6h13M8 4h4M8.5 9v5M11.5 9v5" stroke="currentColor"/></svg></button>
                </div>
            <div class="stage-layers" id="stage-layers" title="Layers &mdash; the active layer receives new geometry">
              <div class="stage-layers-head">Layers</div>
              <div id="layers" class="layers"></div>
              <div class="layer-tools">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-add-layer" title="Add a new layer and make it active">+</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-layer-up" title="Move the highlighted layer up (draws on top)">&#8593;</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-layer-down" title="Move the highlighted layer down (draws below)">&#8595;</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-dim-layers" title="Dim other layers: fade every layer but the active one">&#9680;</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            </div>
      
          
            <div class="stage-colors" id="stage-colors" title="Garis = warna operasi KaryaCNC; kotak = loop fill">
              <div class="swatches" id="swatches"></div>
              <div class="stage-colors-sep" aria-hidden="true"></div>
              <div class="stage-fills" id="loop-fill-row">
                <button class="swatch fill-sq fill-none" data-fill="none" title="No loop fill" aria-label="No loop fill"></button>
                <button class="swatch fill-sq fill-gray" data-fill="gray" title="Gray &mdash; material boundary: everything inside gets flipped" aria-label="Loop fill gray"></button>
                <button class="swatch fill-sq fill-yellow" data-fill="yellow" title="Yellow &mdash; flip the offset direction of this contour" aria-label="Loop fill yellow"></button>
              </div>
            </div>
        <section class="xf-float" id="transform-panel">
          <h3>Transform <span class="count">R</span></h3>
          <div class="xf-fields">
            <label class="xf-field" title="Angle in degrees"><span class="ang" aria-label="Angle">&ang;</span><input id="xf-angle" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" disabled></label>
            <label class="xf-field" title="Scale X"><span>X</span><input id="xf-sx" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" disabled></label>
            <label class="xf-field" title="Scale Y"><span>Y</span><input id="xf-sy" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" disabled></label>
            <label class="tf-lock" id="xf-lock-label" title="Lock width / height ratio" aria-label="Lock width / height ratio">
              <input type="checkbox" id="xf-lock" disabled>
              <svg class="lock-icon" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true">
                <path d="M4.7 7.1V5.2a3.3 3.3 0 0 1 6.6 0v1.9" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.35" stroke-linecap="round"/>
                <rect x="3.3" y="7.1" width="9.4" height="6.4" rx="1.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.35"/>
              </svg>
            </label>
          </div>
          <div class="row">
            <button class="btn small primary" id="xf-apply" disabled>Apply</button>
            <button class="btn small" id="xf-cancel" disabled>Cancel</button>
            <button class="btn small" id="xf-center" title="Move the pivot to the centre of the selection">Center pivot</button>
          </div>
        </section>
          </div>
      
          <div class="vsplit" id="vsplit" role="separator" aria-orientation="vertical"
               aria-label="Resize panels" tabindex="0"></div>
      
          <aside class="sidebar" id="sidebar">
            <section class="panel" id="chat-panel">
              <h3>AI Chat <span class="count" id="chat-count">0</span></h3>
              <div class="row tab-bar" style="margin-bottom:6px">
                <button class="btn small is-on" data-chat-tab="chat">Chat</button>
                <button class="btn small" data-chat-tab="log">Log</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="chat-new" title="Bersihkan percakapan dan mulai chat baru (geometri di worksheet tidak disentuh)">New</button>
              </div>
              <ul class="list" id="chat-log"></ul>
              <div class="row" style="margin-top:6px">
                <input id="chat-input" type="text" placeholder='kotak 100x60 di 0,0…' style="flex:1 1 auto;min-width:0;font:inherit;font-size:11.5px;padding:4px 7px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false">
                <button class="btn small danger hidden" id="chat-stop" title="Hentikan permintaan yang sedang berjalan">■ Stop</button>
                <button class="btn small primary" id="chat-send">Kirim</button>
              </div>
            </section>
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Selection</h3>
              <div id="selection-info" class="selection-info">Nothing selected</div>
              <div class="row">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-name" title="Name the selection (Ctrl+N). A name is only a label: the geometry is not touched; leave it empty to clear">Name</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-to-curve" title="Convert the selected circle or arc to a Bézier curve, or a text object into chained editable lines (one line per outline segment)" disabled>&rarr; Curve</button>
                <button class="btn small toggle" id="btn-group-select" aria-pressed="false" title="Group select: a click takes the whole group. Alt+click always does the opposite.">Group <i class="key">Alt</i></button>
              </div>
              <div class="row">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-group" title="Group the selection into one object (Ctrl+G)">Group</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-ungroup" title="Ungroup: explode grouped/pasted objects back to editable curves (Ctrl+Shift+G)">Ungroup</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-connected" title="Select connected: extend the selection to every shape chained to it (N)">Connected</button>
              </div>
              <div class="constraint-block hidden" id="constraint-block">
                <div class="constraint-head">Constraints <span class="count" id="constraint-count">0</span></div>
                <ul class="list" id="constraint-list"></ul>
              </div>
            </section>
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Parts <span class="count" id="parts-count">0</span></h3>
              <div class="row" style="margin-bottom:6px">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-make-part" title="Bake the selection into a rigid part: one polyline per contour plus a marker on every node and segment midpoint. The master geometry is frozen &mdash; a part only rotates and translates, and dragging one of its markers moves the whole part">Make part</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-mate" title="Mate: click a marker on one part, then a marker on another part (pin &mdash; rotate about the point) or an edge midpoint (slider &mdash; rotate and slide along the line). Clicking the same marker twice grounds that part (Alt+T)">Mate <i class="key">Alt+T</i></button>
              </div>
              <ul class="list" id="parts-list"></ul>
            </section>
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Tools</h3>
              <div class="row" id="tools-row">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-array" title="Clone array: grid or circular copies of the selection. Double click the array to edit its parameters or refresh the clones">Array</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-nest" title="Nesting: with objects selected (plus a 'nest' text/comment) it uploads them to the KaryaCNC nesting server; with only the 'nest' marker — or nothing — selected it downloads the result and asks you to click where to place it">Nest</button>
              </div>
              <div class="constraint-head">Arrays <span class="count" id="array-count">0</span></div>
              <ul class="list" id="array-list"></ul>
            </section>
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Named selections</h3>
              <div class="row" style="margin-bottom:6px">
                <input id="nsel-name" type="text" placeholder="selection name" style="flex:1 1 auto;min-width:0;font:inherit;font-size:11.5px;padding:4px 7px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px">
                <button class="btn small" id="nsel-save">Save</button>
              </div>
              <ul class="list" id="nsel-list"></ul>
            </section>
      
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Constrain</h3>
              <div class="constraint-grid">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-dimension">Dimension <i class="key">D</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="coincident">Coincident <i class="key">O</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="horizontal">Horizontal <i class="key">H</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="vertical">Vertical <i class="key">V</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="parallel">Parallel <i class="key">P</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="perpendicular">Perp <i class="key">[</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="equal">Equal <i class="key">Q</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="anchor">Anchor <i class="key">.</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="midpoint">Midpoint <i class="key">M</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="tangent">Tangent <i class="key">T</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="clear">Clear all</button>
              </div>
                    </section>
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Closed loops <span class="count" id="loop-count">0</span></h3>
              <button class="btn small" id="btn-clean-loops" title="Weld doubled points around every loop: two points that sit within the join tolerance become one point, so each loop is really closed. Works loop by loop, so it stays fast on a big drawing">Clean up loop</button>
              <ul class="list" id="loop-list"></ul>
            </section>
      
            
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <input type="file" id="file-input" accept=".svg,.sketch,.json,application/json,.dxf,.pdf,.cdr,.ai,.eps" hidden>
      <div id="svg-modal" class="modal hidden">
        <div class="modal-box">
          <h3>Export SVG</h3>
          <label class="opt"><input type="checkbox" id="svg-only-selected"> Only selected objects</label>
          <label class="opt"><input type="checkbox" id="svg-only-visible" checked> Only visible layers</label>
          <label class="opt"><input type="checkbox" id="svg-only-active"> Only active layer</label>
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn small" id="svg-cancel">Cancel</button>
            <button class="btn small primary" id="svg-go">Export</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="cnc-modal" class="modal hidden">
        <div class="modal-box cnc-box">
          <h3>Send to KaryaCNC</h3>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Server</span>
            <input id="cnc-host" type="text" spellcheck="false" placeholder="127.0.0.1 or last octet">
            <select id="cnc-port" title="KaryaCNC WebSocket port (wsport)">
              <option value="8885">8885</option>
              <option value="8886">8886</option>
              <option value="8887">8887</option>
              <option value="8888">8888</option>
              <option value="8889">8889</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Send</span>
            <select id="cnc-scope">
              <option value="selected">Selected objects</option>
              <option value="visible">Visible layers</option>
              <option value="active">Active layer only</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Flatten</span>
            <input id="cnc-tol" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" value="0.05">
            <span class="cnc-unit">mm</span>
            <label class="chk cnc-chain" title="Join touching segments into one path (closed contours become cut loops)">
              <input type="checkbox" id="cnc-chain" checked><span>Chain</span>
            </label>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-legend" id="cnc-legend"></div>
          <div class="cnc-status" id="cnc-status">Ready</div>
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn small" id="cnc-cancel">Close</button>
            <button class="btn small primary" id="cnc-send">Send</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="settings-modal" class="modal hidden">
        <div class="settings-box">
          <div class="settings-head">
            <h2>Settings</h2>
            <button class="btn small" id="settings-close">Close</button>
          </div>
          <p class="settings-lead">Klik nama grup untuk membukanya. Keterangan tiap pengaturan muncul sebagai tooltip saat kolomnya difokuskan. Toleransi dalam milimeter; nilai diingat per browser.</p>
          <div class="settings-groups" id="settings-groups"></div>
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn small" id="settings-reset">Reset defaults</button>
            <button class="btn small primary" id="settings-apply">Apply</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div id="image-modal" class="modal hidden">
        <div class="modal-box">
          <h3>Paste image</h3>
          <div class="opt-col">
            <label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="img-mode" id="img-mode-trace" checked> Trace to geometry</label>
            <label class="opt"><input type="radio" name="img-mode" id="img-mode-image"> Keep as image</label>
            <label class="opt">Smoothness (0&ndash;1,34) <input type="number" id="img-smooth" value="1" step="0.05" min="0" max="1.34"></label>
            <label class="opt">Speckle size (px) <input type="number" id="img-turd" value="2" step="1" min="0"></label>
            <label class="opt">Tolerance (px) <input type="number" id="img-tol" value="0.6" step="0.1" min="0.05"></label>
            <label class="opt"><input type="checkbox" id="img-optcurve" checked> Potrace smooth outline (fewer points)</label>
            <label class="opt"><input type="checkbox" id="img-fitcurve" checked> Fit real curves on &#8594; Curve (fewer nodes, corners kept)</label>
            <label class="opt">Pixel density (dpi) <input type="number" id="img-dpi" value="96" step="1" min="1" max="100000"></label>
            <label class="opt">Width (mm) <input type="number" id="img-width" value="100" step="0.01" min="1"></label>
            <div class="opt img-density">Source: <span id="img-dpi-note">&mdash;</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn" id="img-cancel">Cancel</button>
            <button class="btn primary" id="img-ok">OK</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div id="array-modal" class="modal hidden">
        <div class="modal-box">
          <h3>Clone array</h3>
          <label class="opt">Type
            <select id="arr-kind">
              <option value="grid">Grid (rows &times; columns)</option>
              <option value="guide">On Guide Line</option>
            </select>
          </label>
          <div id="arr-grid-fields">
            <label class="opt">Rows <input type="number" id="arr-rows" value="2" min="1" step="1"></label>
            <label class="opt">Columns <input type="number" id="arr-cols" value="2" min="1" step="1"></label>
            <label class="opt">Spacing X (mm) <input type="number" id="arr-dx" value="10" step="0.1"></label>
            <label class="opt">Spacing Y (mm) <input type="number" id="arr-dy" value="-10" step="0.1"></label>
          </div>
          <div id="arr-guide-fields" style="display:none">
            <label class="opt">Count <input type="number" id="arr-count" value="6" min="2" step="1"></label>
            <label class="opt"><input type="checkbox" id="arr-rotate"> Rotate clone on guide</label>
            <div class="cnc-status">Select a green polyline or curve as the guide alongside your master object.</div>
          </div>
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn small" id="arr-cancel">Cancel</button>
            <button class="btn small primary" id="arr-run">OK</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="cdr-import-modal" class="modal hidden">
        <div class="modal-box">
          <h3>Import CDR</h3>
          <span class="imp-name" id="cdr-imp-file"></span>
          <label class="opt">Text objects &rarr;
            <select id="cdr-text-mode">
              <option value="comment">comments</option>
              <option value="text">text objects</option>
              <option value="skip">skip</option>
            </select>
          </label>
          <label class="opt"><input type="checkbox" id="cdr-keep-color"> Keep original colours (off = snap each line to the nearest MiniSketch colour, fills dropped)</label>
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn small" id="cdr-imp-cancel">Cancel</button>
            <button class="btn small primary" id="cdr-imp-run">Import</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="svg-import-modal" class="modal hidden">
        <div class="modal-box imp-box">
          <div class="imp-head">
            <h3>Import SVG</h3>
            <span class="imp-name" id="imp-file"></span>
          </div>
          <p class="imp-summary" id="imp-summary"></p>
          <p class="imp-warn" id="imp-warn" hidden></p>
      
          <label class="imp-opt">
            <input type="checkbox" id="imp-keep-groups" checked>
            <span>Keep each group as one object <em>&mdash; safer for a customer file; Ctrl+Shift+G breaks it apart</em></span>
          </label>
      
          <label class="imp-opt">
            <span>Filled areas</span>
            <select id="imp-fill">
              <option value="gray" selected>Gray &middot; material boundary</option>
              <option value="yellow">Yellow &middot; flip the offset</option>
              <option value="ignore">Ignore the fill</option>
            </select>
          </label>
      
          <div class="imp-sub">Colours in the file &rarr; KaryaCNC operation</div>
          <div class="imp-colors" id="imp-colors"></div>
      
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn small" id="imp-cancel">Cancel</button>
            <button class="btn small primary" id="imp-run">Import</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div id="help-modal" class="modal hidden">
        <div class="modal-box help-box">
          <div class="help-head">
            <h3>Help</h3>
            <div class="help-lang" role="group" aria-label="Language">
              <button type="button" class="btn small is-on" data-help-lang="en">English</button>
              <button type="button" class="btn small" data-help-lang="id">Bahasa Indonesia</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="help-body" id="help-body">
            <div class="help-pane" data-help-pane="en">
            <!-- help content lives in help.js -->
          </div>
            <div class="help-pane" data-help-pane="id" hidden>
            <!-- help content lives in help.js -->
          </div>
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn small" id="help-close">Close</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      </div>
      <div id="text-modal" class="modal hidden">
        <div class="modal-box text-box">
          <h3>Text</h3>
          <div class="cnc-row text-row-stack">
            <span class="cnc-label">Content</span>
            <textarea id="text-value" rows="3" spellcheck="false"></textarea>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Font</span>
            <select id="text-font"></select>
            <button class="btn small" id="text-import" title="Add a font file (.ttf / .otf / .woff) from disk — it is stored in this browser and shows up under Imported">Import font&hellip;</button>
            <input type="file" id="text-font-input" accept=".ttf,.otf,.woff,.woff2" multiple hidden>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Style</span>
            <select id="text-weight">
              <option value="300">Light</option>
              <option value="400">Regular</option>
              <option value="600">Semibold</option>
              <option value="700">Bold</option>
            </select>
            <select id="text-italic">
              <option value="0">Upright</option>
              <option value="1">Italic</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Height</span>
            <input id="text-height" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" value="20">
            <span class="cnc-unit">mm</span>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Line spacing</span>
            <input id="text-leading" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" value="125">
            <span class="cnc-unit">%</span>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Align</span>
            <select id="text-align">
              <option value="left">Left</option>
              <option value="center">Center</option>
              <option value="right">Right</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Tracking</span>
            <input id="text-tracking" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" value="0">
            <span class="cnc-unit">%</span>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Detail</span>
            <input id="text-tol" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" value="0.08">
            <span class="cnc-unit">mm</span>
          </div>
          <label class="opt"><input type="checkbox" id="text-fitcurve" checked> Fit real curves on &#8594; Curve (fewer nodes, sharp corners kept)</label>
          <div class="cnc-status">Double-click the text any time to edit. Dimension the green handles: one dimension keeps the glyph ratio, two release it. <b>Detail</b> is the outline accuracy in mm (0.08 default; larger = fewer points). Send traces the glyphs automatically. With <b>Fit real curves</b> on, &#8594; Curve rebuilds the glyphs as a few real Bezier curves; with it off you get one straight line per contour segment instead. A <b>Single-line</b> font is a stroke font: &rarr; Curve and Send produce open paths, one per stem — what engraving / V-carving wants.</div>
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn small" id="text-curve" title="Replace the text object with editable geometry — closed curves per glyph outline, or open splines per stroke for a Single-line font">&#8594; Curve</button>
            <button class="btn small" id="text-cancel">Cancel</button>
            <button class="btn small primary" id="text-apply">Apply</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="fit-modal" class="modal hidden">
        <div class="modal-box fit-box">
          <h3>Fit curve</h3>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Tolerance</span>
            <input id="fit-tol" type="range" min="0.01" max="2" step="0.01" value="0.2">
            <span class="cnc-unit" id="fit-tol-val">0.2 mm</span>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-row">
            <span class="cnc-label">Sharp corner</span>
            <input id="fit-sharp" type="range" min="0" max="90" step="1" value="25">
            <span class="cnc-unit" id="fit-sharp-val">25&deg;</span>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-status" id="fit-info"></div>
          <div class="cnc-status">Lower <b>Tolerance</b> follows the shape more closely with more nodes; a lower <b>Sharp corner</b> keeps more turns as corners. Press Ctrl+K again on the result to re-fit it.</div>
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn small" id="fit-cancel">Cancel</button>
            <button class="btn small primary" id="fit-apply">Apply</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div id="mate-modal" class="modal hidden">
        <div class="modal-box">
          <h3>Mate parts</h3>
          <div class="opt-col">
            <label class="opt">Type
              <select id="mate-type">
                <option value="pin">Pin &mdash; rotate about the point</option>
                <option value="slider">Slider &mdash; slide + rotate on the line</option>
                <option value="string">String &mdash; stretchy thread, shows length change</option>
                <option value="arc">Arc &mdash; angular travel of side B around side A</option>
              </select>
            </label>
            <div class="cnc-row"><span class="cnc-label">Side A</span><span id="mate-side-a" class="cnc-unit mate-side">&mdash;</span></div>
            <div class="cnc-row"><span class="cnc-label">Side B</span><span id="mate-side-b" class="cnc-unit mate-side">&mdash;</span></div>
            <label class="opt" id="mate-ground-row">
              <input type="checkbox" id="mate-ground">
              Weld &mdash; make side A the grounded base
            </label>
          </div>
          <div class="cnc-status" id="mate-status">A pin glues the two markers together (rotation stays free). A slider keeps a marker on the other part's edge: it may rotate and slide along that line.</div>
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn" id="mate-cancel">Cancel</button>
            <button class="btn primary" id="mate-ok">Mate</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <script src="fonts/fonts.js"></script>
      <script src="fonts/hershey-data.js"></script>
      <script src="fonts/hershey.js"></script>
      <script src="help.js"></script>
        <script src="tools/Potrace.js"></script>
      <script src="tools/clipper.js"></script>
      <script src="tools/path-bool.js"></script>
      <script src="tools/registry.js"></script>
      <script src="tools/box.js"></script>
      <script src="tools/slot.js"></script>
      <script src="tools/gear.js"></script>
      <script src="tools/wall.js"></script>
      <script src="tools/hinge.js"></script>
      <script src="tools/slidetop.js"></script>
      <script src="tools/pulltop.js"></script>
      <script src="tools/paintrack.js"></script>
      <script src="ai/chat.js"></script>
      <script src="core.js"></script>
      <script src="app.js"></script>
      <div id="toast" class="toast"></div>
      </body>
      </html>
      
- path: app.js
  content: |
      (() => {
        'use strict';
      
        // MiniSketch application shell. Pure math/geometry lives in core.js (loaded
        // first, see ARCHITECTURE.md for the data model and the invariants that must
        // not be broken); the Tools-panel generators live in tools/*.js and reach
        // this file only through the toolApi() surface. Rough map, top to bottom:
        //
        //   constants + colour codes      document model (blankState, P, entityPointIds)
        //   SVG import analysis           layer helpers + pointUses index
        //   settings (SETTINGS_SPEC)      view / camera (w2s, s2w, fitView)
        //   theme                         history + persistence + serializeState
        //   UI shell (blankState/ui)      loops (getLoops, invalidateLoops)
        //   solver wiring (solveSketch)   constraint conflicts + commands (cmd*)
        //   transform mode (xf*)          copy / paste
        //   dimension geometry            drawing (render, draw*)
        //   hit-testing                   panels / menus / keyboard
        //   KaryaCNC send                 SVG import insert / export
        //   tools panel (toolApi/mount)   layers panel / loadDocument / download / init
        //
        // Two rules worth repeating here because every perf bug so far came from
        // breaking them: never scan all entities per point (use pointUses), and the
        // only "geometry changed" signal is invalidateLoops().
      
        const TAU = Math.PI * 2;
        // A solve may change a radius by at most this factor in either direction.
        // Without it a nearly singular system could push a circle from r=3 to r=300
        // in one go; 2x is generous enough for any real constraint.
        const RADIUS_SOLVE_LIMIT = 2;
        const STORAGE_KEY = 'minisketch.v1';
        const VIEW_KEY = 'minisketch.v1.view';
        const LAYOUT_KEY = 'minisketch.v1.layout';
        const FORMAT = 'minisketch';
        const VERSION = 2;
        const EPS = 1e-9;
        // Line colours are KaryaCNC stroke codes: the colour IS the operation.
        const COLORS = [
          { name: 'Cut (black)', value: '#000000' },
          { name: 'Engrave (blue)', value: '#0000ff' },
          { name: 'V-carve (cyan)', value: '#00ffff' },
          { name: 'Pocket (purple)', value: '#800080' },
          { name: 'Drill (red)', value: '#ff0000' },
          { name: 'Mark (green)', value: '#00ff00' },
          { name: 'Cut tab (maroon)', value: '#800000' },
          { name: 'Start point (teal)', value: '#008080' }
        ];
      
        // the hex codes on their own, for the import colour mapping
        const CNC_CODES = COLORS.map(c => c.value);
      
        // Above this many entities the renderer switches to its cheap path (colour
        // batching, no point handles), which is what keeps an imported drawing usable.
        const BULK_ENTITIES = 1200;
      
        // The CNC tolerance, in millimetres: whatever is closer than this is one joint.
        // It decides when a selection walks on (N), when an imported path counts as
        // closed, and when two ends of a contour make a loop.
        let JOIN_TOL = 0.1;
        let NUDGE_STEP = 1;               // mm per arrow key press (Settings)
        let NOTE_SIZE = 3;                // comment text height, mm (Settings)
        let NOTE_COLOR = '#ffcc00';       // one colour for all comments (Settings)
        let PART_WIDTH = 2;               // part stroke width, px (Settings)
        // Part stroke: dark green on the light canvas; CANVAS_DARK translates it to
        // a light green in the dark theme (see drawPartEntity).
        const PART_COLOR = '#15803d';
      
        const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas');
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        const stage = document.getElementById('stage');
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('file-input');
      
        // ---- Theme -------------------------------------------------------------
        // UI colours live in styles.css (--tokens per data-theme). The canvas is
        // painted with light-theme literals all over the renderer, so instead of
        // touching every draw call the ctx colour setters are wrapped and the
        // literals are translated for the dark palette by CANVAS_DARK.
        const THEME_KEY = 'minisketch.theme';
        const CANVAS_DARK_RAW = {
          // canvas surface, handles, label/badge fills
          '#ffffff': '#11161d',
          '#fff': '#11161d',
          'rgba(255,255,255,0.92)': 'rgba(23,29,37,0.92)',
          // accents / selection
          '#2563eb': '#4f8cff',
          '#1d4ed8': '#8ab4ff',
          '#7b8798': '#9aa8ba',
          '#7c3aed': '#a78bfa',
          '#f59e0b': '#fbbf24',
          // geometry neutrals
          '#111111': '#e6ebf2',
          '#000000': '#e6ebf2',
          '#8b929c': '#9aa4b2',
          '#16a34a': '#4ade80',
          '#dc2626': '#f87171',
          // KaryaCNC operation colours, brightened for a dark canvas
          '#0000ff': '#6b9bff',
          '#00ffff': '#33d6e6',
          '#800080': '#c084fc',
          '#ff0000': '#ff6b6b',
          '#00ff00': '#4ade80',
          '#800000': '#e08080',
          '#008080': '#2dd4bf',
          // part stroke (light-theme dark green -> light green on the dark canvas)
          '#15803d': '#4ade80',
          '#3f4856': '#c9d3e0',
          '#d5dbe3': '#39424f',
          '#cbd5e1': '#3a4452',
          '#64748b': '#9aa6b6',
          '#4b5563': '#9aa4b2',
          '#111827': '#e6ebf2',
          '#5b6573': '#a7b2c2',
          // grid / axes / labels
          '#c7ced8': '#2c3541',
          '#e4e8ee': '#242c36',
          '#eef1f5': '#1b222a',
          '#a3acb9': '#5c6675',
          '#9aa4b2': '#5c6675',
          '#b6bec9': '#4e5866',
          '#dbe6ff': '#1b3a66',
          // translucent accents
          'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.95)': 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.95)',
          'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.8)': 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.8)',
          'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.75)': 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.75)',
          'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.7)': 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.7)',
          'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.55)': 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.55)',
          'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.5)': 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.55)',
          'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.16)': 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.18)',
          'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.10)': 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.12)',
          'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.08)': 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.10)',
          'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.05)': 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.07)',
          'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.9)': 'rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.9)',
          'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.85)': 'rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.85)',
          'rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.62)': 'rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.55)',
          'rgba(185, 28, 28, 0.9)': 'rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.95)',
          'rgba(22, 163, 74, 0.62)': 'rgba(74, 222, 128, 0.5)',
          'rgba(22, 163, 74, 0.22)': 'rgba(74, 222, 128, 0.2)',
          'rgba(22, 163, 74, 0.045)': 'rgba(74, 222, 128, 0.05)',
          'rgba(21, 128, 61, 0.9)': 'rgba(74, 222, 128, 0.95)'
        };
        const CANVAS_DARK = Object.create(null);
        for (const key in CANVAS_DARK_RAW) {
          CANVAS_DARK[key.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '')] = CANVAS_DARK_RAW[key];
        }
        let themeName = 'dark';
      
        function themedCanvasColor(v) {
          if (themeName !== 'dark') return v;
          return CANVAS_DARK[v.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '')] || v;
        }
      
        function themeCanvas() {
          const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(ctx);
          for (const prop of ['strokeStyle', 'fillStyle']) {
            const desc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(proto, prop);
            if (!desc || !desc.set) continue;
            let current;
            Object.defineProperty(ctx, prop, {
              configurable: true,
              get() { return current; },
              set(v) {
                current = typeof v === 'string' ? themedCanvasColor(v) : v;
                desc.set.call(ctx, current);
              }
            });
          }
        }
      
        function applyTheme(name, opts) {
          themeName = name === 'light' ? 'light' : 'dark';
          document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', themeName);
          if (!opts || opts.persist !== false) {
            try { localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, themeName); } catch (err) { /* ignore */ }
          }
          const btn = document.getElementById('btn-theme');
          if (btn) {
            btn.textContent = themeName === 'dark' ? '\u2600' : '\u263e';
            btn.title = themeName === 'dark' ? 'Switch to light theme' : 'Switch to dark theme';
            btn.setAttribute('aria-label', btn.title);
          }
          if (!opts || opts.render !== false) render();
        }
      
        function toggleTheme() {
          const next = themeName === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
          applyTheme(next);
          try {
            if (window.parent && window.parent !== window)
              window.parent.postMessage({ type: 'karyacnc-set-theme', theme: next }, '*');
          } catch (err) { /* ignore */ }
        }
      
      
        // ---- Import: SVG ----------------------------------------------------------
        // The model has no nested transforms, so an imported tree is flattened: the
        // group chain is composed into world coordinates while walking. Structure is
        // kept cheaply instead: layers, a flat group tag, or one framed object per
        // group ("keep each group as one object").
        const SVG_MM_PER = { mm: 1, cm: 10, m: 1000, in: 25.4, pt: 25.4 / 72, pc: 25.4 / 6, px: 25.4 / 96, q: 0.25 };
      
        function svgLenToMm(raw) {
          if (raw == null) return null;
          const m = String(raw).trim().match(/^(-?[\d.]+(?:e[-+]?\d+)?)\s*([a-z%]*)$/i);
          if (!m) return null;
          const n = parseFloat(m[1]);
          if (!isFinite(n)) return null;
          const unit = (m[2] || 'px').toLowerCase();
          if (unit === '%') return null;
          const k = SVG_MM_PER[unit];
          return k == null ? null : n * k;
        }
      
        // --- colour -----------------------------------------------------------------
        const SVG_NAMED = {
          black: '#000000', white: '#ffffff', red: '#ff0000', green: '#008000', lime: '#00ff00',
          blue: '#0000ff', yellow: '#ffff00', cyan: '#00ffff', aqua: '#00ffff', magenta: '#ff00ff',
          fuchsia: '#ff00ff', gray: '#808080', grey: '#808080', silver: '#c0c0c0', maroon: '#800000',
          olive: '#808000', navy: '#000080', purple: '#800080', teal: '#008080', orange: '#ffa500'
        };
      
        function svgColorToHex(str) {
          if (str == null) return null;
          const v = String(str).trim().toLowerCase();
          if (!v || v === 'none' || v === 'transparent') return null;
          if (v[0] === '#') {
            const h = v.slice(1);
            if (h.length === 3) return '#' + h[0] + h[0] + h[1] + h[1] + h[2] + h[2];
            if (h.length === 6) return '#' + h;
            return null;
          }
          const m = v.match(/^rgba?\(([^)]+)\)$/);
          if (m) {
            const parts = m[1].split(/[,/\s]+/).filter(Boolean).map(parseFloat);
            if (parts.length >= 3) {
              if (parts.length > 3 && parts[3] === 0) return null;
              const hex = parts.slice(0, 3).map(n => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(n))).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
              return '#' + hex;
            }
          }
          if (SVG_NAMED[v]) return SVG_NAMED[v];
          if (/^url\(/.test(v)) return null;                       // gradient / pattern
          return null;
        }
      
        function hexToRgb(hex) {
          const h = String(hex || '').replace('#', '');
          if (h.length !== 6) return null;
          return [parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16), parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16), parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16)];
        }
      
        // sRGB -> CIE Lab (D65), so "nearest" matches the eye and not the raw numbers.
        function rgbToLab(r, g, b) {
          const lin = (c) => {
            const s = c / 255;
            return s <= 0.04045 ? s / 12.92 : Math.pow((s + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
          };
          const R = lin(r), G = lin(g), B = lin(b);
          const X = (R * 0.4124 + G * 0.3576 + B * 0.1805) / 0.95047;
          const Y = (R * 0.2126 + G * 0.7152 + B * 0.0722) / 1.0;
          const Z = (R * 0.0193 + G * 0.1192 + B * 0.9505) / 1.08883;
          const f = (t) => (t > 0.008856 ? Math.cbrt(t) : (7.787 * t + 16 / 116));
          return [116 * f(Y) - 16, 500 * (f(X) - f(Y)), 200 * (f(Y) - f(Z))];
        }
      
        function colorDistanceLab(a, b) {
          const A = rgbToLab(a[0], a[1], a[2]);
          const B = rgbToLab(b[0], b[1], b[2]);
          return Math.hypot(A[0] - B[0], A[1] - B[1], A[2] - B[2]);
        }
      
        // Nearest KaryaCNC stroke code; ties fall back to black (cut).
        function nearestCncColor(hex) {
          const rgb = hexToRgb(hex);
          if (!rgb) return '#000000';
          let best = '#000000';
          let bestD = Infinity;
          for (const code of CNC_CODES) {
            const c = hexToRgb(code);
            if (!c) continue;
            const d = colorDistanceLab(rgb, c);
            if (d < bestD - 1e-9) { bestD = d; best = code; }
          }
          return best;
        }
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
        // --- style -------------------------------------------------------------------
        function svgStyleDecls(text) {
          const out = {};
          for (const part of String(text || '').split(';')) {
            const at = part.indexOf(':');
            if (at < 0) continue;
            const k = part.slice(0, at).trim().toLowerCase();
            const v = part.slice(at + 1).trim();
            if (k) out[k] = v;
          }
          return out;
        }
      
        // <style> blocks: only simple ".class { }" / "tag { }" rules are honoured.

      Line 28:       { en: "<p><b>Trim (I)</b> cuts at crossings: click a span and it goes, the rest of the object stays. <b>Ctrl</b>+click instead walks the chain &mdash; the whole run of touching lines, arcs and curves is cut at once, from the clicked span out to the next crossing. <b>Separate (E)</b> splits the same way but deletes nothing, so it is Trim without the loss. A closed curve has no ends to walk from, so a plain click splits it at every crossing. With no crossing under the click the tool says so instead of eating the shape.</p>"
      Line 31:       { en: "<p>The <b>curve pen</b> draws cubic B&eacute;ziers: click for a corner, click-and-drag for a smooth bend, click the first node to close it. <b>Enter</b>, a double click or a right click finishes the curve; <b>Esc</b> cancels. The <b>poly</b> tool asks for a centre first, then the radius and start angle; the wheel sets the star inner percentage, <b>Ctrl</b>+wheel the number of sides.</p>"
      Line 34:       { en: "<p>The <b>line</b> and <b>curve</b> tools share one set of drafting keys: <b>Enter</b> finishes the shape, <b>Esc</b> takes the last line / node back (press it again to stop drafting altogether), holding <b>Shift</b> tracks the polar axis in 15&deg; steps from the previous point, and holding <b>Alt</b> inverts the Snap toggle for as long as it is held. The preview follows the same rules, so what you see is what you get. Both tools also stop on their own when the chain closes: click the point you started from and the loop is complete.</p>"
      Line 37:       { en: "<p>Double-click a line or an arc to <b>split</b> it: the first half keeps the original id, so its constraints stay attached. Hold <b>Shift</b> and double-click instead to only drop an on-line point without splitting. Double-click a curve to add a node, or a dimension label to change its value. Click a curve (or one of its nodes) to show its <b>control handles</b>; drag a knob to reshape the curve like in Inkscape. Click inside a closed loop to select its shape; <b>Ctrl</b>+click takes the loop and everything it encloses, <b>Shift</b>+click toggles the loop's shape in and out of the selection. When loops overlap, <b>Alt</b>+click (or the <b>middle button</b>, handy on Linux where the desktop steals Alt) takes the loop below &mdash; repeat to step further down. Drag a rectangle to select many; <b>Shift</b> (or <b>Ctrl</b>) adds to the selection and <b>Alt</b> takes the rectangle's contents back out. Drag a dimension label to slide it aside.</p>"
      Line 40:       { en: "<p><b>J</b> joins selected points that touch (with closed loops selected it welds the doubled points around each loop, and it asks first when more than 100 points are selected); <b>F</b> fillets two lines or the corner points you selected. <b>&rarr; Curve</b> turns a circle, an arc, a text or a pasted picture into plain editable curves and lines. <b>;</b> drops a comment at the cursor and asks for its text right away; drag it anywhere, double click to edit. Comments are one size and one colour (set in Settings) and never go to KaryaCNC, grouping or offset.</p>"
      Line 72:       { en: "<p>The <b>L / T / W / H</b> fields in the top bar set the bounds of the selection directly; click the small <b>padlock</b> next to them to keep the proportions while resizing.</p>"

      Line 102:       { en: "<p><b>W</b> places a text object: type the content, pick a font, weight, italic and tracking in the dialog, then double-click the text later to reopen it. The object has three points and two green guide lines (start, width, height). Green means travel-only in KaryaCNC, so the guides are never cut; otherwise they behave like normal lines and take H/V, angle, perpendicular and dimension constraints.</p>"
      Line 108:       { en: "<p>The font list is grouped by what the font is <i>for</i>: <b>Single-line (engrave)</b>, then Sans, Serif, Monospace and <b>Imported</b>. <b>Import font&hellip;</b> adds any <code>.ttf</code> / <code>.otf</code> / <code>.woff</code> from disk — or drop the file on the canvas. It is stored inside this browser only (IndexedDB), never in the system font folder and never uploaded. A bundled family loads the first time you pick it.</p><p><b>Single-line</b> fonts (Hershey Simplex and Script) are stroke fonts: each glyph is a set of open polylines, which is what engraving and V-carving want. They draw, trace and send as open paths — one per stem, no closed loops — and <b>&rarr; Curve</b> fits them as open splines. <b>Line spacing</b> is a percentage of the font size (125 is the default) and <b>Align</b> sets left, centre or right for every line inside the text box.</p>"
      Line 131:       { en: "<p><b>Ctrl+V</b> with an image on the clipboard (a screenshot, a PNG, a JPG), or drop an image file on the canvas: it is traced locally with Potrace and arrives as one object with the same three points and two green guides as a text.       Line 144:       { en: "<p><b>Ctrl+O</b> offsets: you get a dashed live preview, the wheel changes the distance (Shift = 0.1&nbsp;mm, Alt = 5&nbsp;mm), <b>Enter</b> asks for an exact value and confirms it, <b>Esc</b> cancels. Closed shapes go through Clipper, so sharp corners stay clean and never leave crossing lines; whether corners come out sharp, rounded or squared is a setting.       Line 160:       { en: "<p><b>Loop fill</b>: click inside a closed loop (or a row in Closed loops) and pick <b>None</b>, <b>Gray</b> or <b>Yellow</b>. Marker&nbsp;&rarr;&nbsp;line is a <b>slider</b> (purple) — the point rides that edge and can slide along it. Clicking the same marker twice is a <b>weld</b> (red), and ticking <b>ground</b> (red triangle) pins a marker to that spot in the world with rotation still free. The mate list under the parts shows every joint, each with its own <b>&times;</b> to remove it; hovering a mate row lights both parts.</p>"
      Line 206:       { en: "<ul class=\"help-list\"><li><b>Box maker</b> and <b>Gear maker</b> (Tools panel): finger joint boxes with inside/outside sizing, and spur gears, racks and pulleys built from flat stock \u2014 N body layers plus two flanges, shaft hole, set screws. The result is one group; double clicking it reopens the generator with its parameters, and the group name shows at its top-left corner. Selecting a comment (<b>;</b>) together with shapes and pressing <b>Ctrl+G</b> puts the comment inside the group &mdash; it travels with it and comes back as a comment when the group is broken apart.</li><li><b>Hinge box</b> (Tools panel) lays out six panels with <b>round knuckle corners</b>: the front and back walls carry an upright slot near each end, and the side walls roll round a knuckle (a barrel with a pin hole) whose prong passes through that slot &mdash; the corners hinge instead of being finger jointed. The horizontal edges keep their finger-joint tabs into the base and lid, and the lid has a lift scoop. Parameters are the box size and the stock thickness; every joint size follows the stock.</li><li><b>Extrude wall</b> (Tools panel) stands the closed loops up as a flat-pack set: a <b>base</b> with finger joint notches, a <b>lid</b> with an engraved guide, a <b>lid plug</b>, and one bendable <b>wall strip</b> per loop. Every joint dimension follows the stock: the finger is 2&times; the thickness long and one thickness wide, so its slot measures (length + kerf) &times; (thickness + kerf). A finger is only placed where the bend radius is at least 25&nbsp;mm (a tighter bend butt-joins instead). The hinge appears only where the outline curves (straight runs stay solid) and alternates two bridge patterns so the strip folds both ways. Double clicking the group reopens it.</li><li><b>Slide top</b> (Tools panel) builds a box whose <b>lid slides in a groove</b> instead of being finger jointed down: the base and the four walls keep their finger-joint tabs, the two side walls carry a red <b>glue mark</b> near the top, and two <b>rails</b> are glued on to form the groove. The <b>slide</b> has a bowed leading edge and a tongue that runs in that groove; eight panels in all. Every joint follows the stock thickness, and the group reopens on a double click.</li></ul>"

                if (k.stroke != null) style.stroke = k.stroke;
                if (k.fill != null) { style.fill = k.fill; style.fillSet = true; }
                if (k['stroke-width'] != null) style.strokeWidth = k['stroke-width'];
                if (k['fill-rule'] != null) style.fillRule = k['fill-rule'];
                if (k.display != null) style.display = k.display;
                if (k.visibility != null) style.visibility = k.visibility;
                if (k.opacity != null) style.opacity = k.opacity;
              }
            }
          }
        }
      
        function svgStyleFor(el, parent, rules) {
          const st = {
            stroke: parent.stroke, fill: parent.fill, fillSet: !!parent.fillSet,
            strokeWidth: parent.strokeWidth, fillRule: parent.fillRule,
            display: parent.display, visibility: parent.visibility, opacity: parent.opacity
          };
          const attr = (k) => (el.getAttribute && el.hasAttribute && el.hasAttribute(k) ? el.getAttribute(k) : null);
          const keys = {
            stroke: 'stroke', fill: 'fill', 'stroke-width': 'strokeWidth',
            'fill-rule': 'fillRule', display: 'display', visibility: 'visibility', opacity: 'opacity'
          };
          for (const k in keys) {
            const v = attr(k);
            if (v != null) {
              st[keys[k]] = v;
              if (keys[k] === 'fill') st.fillSet = true;
            }
          }
          if (rules && rules.length) svgApplyRules(rules, el, st);
          const inline = attr('style');
          if (inline) {
            const d = svgStyleDecls(inline);
            if (d.stroke != null) st.stroke = d.stroke;
            if (d.fill != null) st.fill = d.fill;
            if (d.fill != null) st.fillSet = true;
            if (d['stroke-width'] != null) st.strokeWidth = d['stroke-width'];
            if (d['fill-rule'] != null) st.fillRule = d['fill-rule'];
            if (d.display != null) st.display = d.display;
            if (d.visibility != null) st.visibility = d.visibility;
            if (d.opacity != null) st.opacity = d.opacity;
          }
          return st;
        }
      
        function svgHidden(style) {
          if (String(style.display || '').toLowerCase() === 'none') return true;
          if (String(style.visibility || '').toLowerCase() === 'hidden') return true;
          if (style.opacity != null && parseFloat(style.opacity) === 0) return true;
          return false;
        }
      
        // --- tree walker -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Everything lands in ctx.items as { subpaths, color, filled, group, clip } with
        // world coordinates already baked.
        function svgLocalId(el) {
          return el && el.getAttribute ? (el.getAttribute('id') || '') : '';
        }
      
        function svgHref(el) {
          const raw = (el.getAttribute && (el.getAttribute('href') || el.getAttribute('xlink:href'))) || '';
          return raw[0] === '#' ? raw.slice(1) : '';
        }
      
        function svgPointsAttr(raw) {
          const nums = String(raw || '').match(/-?[\d.]+(?:e[-+]?\d+)?/g);
          if (!nums) return [];
          const pts = [];
          for (let i = 0; i + 1 < nums.length; i += 2) pts.push({ x: +nums[i], y: +nums[i + 1] });
          return pts;
        }
      
        function svgRoundRectSegs(x, y, w, h, rx, ry) {
          const segs = [];
          if (!(w > 0) || !(h > 0)) return segs;
          rx = Math.max(0, Math.min(rx == null ? 0 : rx, w / 2));
          ry = Math.max(0, Math.min(ry == null ? rx : ry, h / 2));
          segs.push({ t: 'M', p: { x: x + rx, y } });
          if (rx > 0 && ry > 0) {
            segs.push({ t: 'L', p: { x: x + w - rx, y } });
            segs.push(...svgArcToCubics(x + w - rx, y, rx, ry, 0, false, true, x + w, y + ry));
            segs.push({ t: 'L', p: { x: x + w, y: y + h - ry } });
            segs.push(...svgArcToCubics(x + w, y + h - ry, rx, ry, 0, false, true, x + w - rx, y + h));
            segs.push({ t: 'L', p: { x: x + rx, y: y + h } });
            segs.push(...svgArcToCubics(x + rx, y + h, rx, ry, 0, false, true, x, y + h - ry));
            segs.push({ t: 'L', p: { x, y: y + ry } });
            segs.push(...svgArcToCubics(x, y + ry, rx, ry, 0, false, true, x + rx, y));
          } else {
            segs.push({ t: 'L', p: { x: x + w, y } }, { t: 'L', p: { x: x + w, y: y + h } },
              { t: 'L', p: { x, y: y + h } });
          }
          segs.push({ t: 'Z' });
          return segs;
        }
      
        function svgEllipseSegs(cx, cy, rx, ry) {
          const k = 0.5522847498307936;
          const ox = rx * k, oy = ry * k;
          return [
            { t: 'M', p: { x: cx + rx, y: cy } },
            { t: 'C', from: { x: cx + rx, y: cy }, c1: { x: cx + rx, y: cy + oy }, c2: { x: cx + ox, y: cy + ry }, p: { x: cx, y: cy + ry } },
            { t: 'C', from: { x: cx, y: cy + ry }, c1: { x: cx - ox, y: cy + ry }, c2: { x: cx - rx, y: cy + oy }, p: { x: cx - rx, y: cy } },
            { t: 'C', from: { x: cx - rx, y: cy }, c1: { x: cx - rx, y: cy - oy }, c2: { x: cx - ox, y: cy - ry }, p: { x: cx, y: cy - ry } },
            { t: 'C', from: { x: cx, y: cy - ry }, c1: { x: cx + ox, y: cy - ry }, c2: { x: cx + rx, y: cy - oy }, p: { x: cx + rx, y: cy } },
            { t: 'Z' }
          ];
        }
      
        function svgShapeSegs(el) {
          const tag = String(el.nodeName || '').toLowerCase();
          const a = (k, d) => {
            const v = el.getAttribute(k);
            if (v == null || v === '') return d;
            const n = parseFloat(v);
            return isFinite(n) ? n : d;
          };
          if (tag === 'path') return svgPathSegments(el.getAttribute('d') || '');
          if (tag === 'polyline' || tag === 'polygon') {
            const pts = svgPointsAttr(el.getAttribute('points'));
            if (pts.length < 2) return [];
            const segs = [{ t: 'M', p: pts[0] }];
            for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) segs.push({ t: 'L', p: pts[i] });
            if (tag === 'polygon') segs.push({ t: 'Z' });
            return segs;
          }
          if (tag === 'line') {
            return [{ t: 'M', p: { x: a('x1', 0), y: a('y1', 0) } }, { t: 'L', p: { x: a('x2', 0), y: a('y2', 0) } }];
          }
          if (tag === 'rect') {
            const rxAttr = el.hasAttribute('rx') ? a('rx', 0) : null;
            const ryAttr = el.hasAttribute('ry') ? a('ry', 0) : null;
            return svgRoundRectSegs(a('x', 0), a('y', 0), a('width', 0), a('height', 0),
              rxAttr == null ? ryAttr : rxAttr, ryAttr == null ? rxAttr : ryAttr);
          }
          if (tag === 'ellipse') return svgEllipseSegs(a('cx', 0), a('cy', 0), a('rx', 0), a('ry', 0));
          if (tag === 'circle') { const r = a('r', 0); return svgEllipseSegs(a('cx', 0), a('cy', 0), r, r); }
          if (tag === 'text' || tag === 'tspan' || tag === 'textpath') return [];
          return [];
        }
      
        const SVG_SHAPES = { path: 1, polyline: 1, polygon: 1, line: 1, rect: 1, circle: 1, ellipse: 1 };
        const SVG_CONTAINERS = { g: 1, svg: 1, a: 1, switch: 1, symbol: 1, defs: 1, clippath: 1, marker: 1, mask: 1, pattern: 1 };
      
        function svgWalk(el, ctx, ctm, parentStyle, groupTag, depth) {
          if (!el || depth > 40) return;
          const tag = String(el.nodeName || '').toLowerCase();
          if (tag === 'title' || tag === 'desc' || tag === 'metadata' || tag === 'style') return;
      
          const style = svgStyleFor(el, parentStyle, ctx.rules);
          if (svgHidden(style)) return;
      
          let local = parseTransformList(el.getAttribute && el.getAttribute('transform'));
          if (tag === 'use') {
            const x = parseFloat(el.getAttribute('x') || '0') || 0;
            const y = parseFloat(el.getAttribute('y') || '0') || 0;
            local = matMul(local, [1, 0, 0, 1, x, y]);
          }
          const m = matMul(ctm, local);
      
          // a group may name itself, that is what the group tag / framed object uses
          let group = groupTag;
          if (tag === 'g' && !group) {
            ctx.groupSeq++;
            group = ctx.prefix + 'g' + ctx.groupSeq;
            const label = (el.getAttribute('inkscape:label') || el.getAttribute('id') || '').trim();
            ctx.groupNames[group] = label;
          }
      
          let clip = null;
          const clipAttr = el.getAttribute && el.getAttribute('clip-path');
          if (clipAttr) clip = svgResolveClip(clipAttr, ctx, m, el);
      
          if (SVG_SHAPES[tag]) {
            const strokeHex = svgColorToHex(style.stroke);
            const fillHex = svgColorToHex(style.fill);
            const filled = !!fillHex && style.fillSet === true;
            const color = strokeHex || fillHex || '#000000';
            const num = (k, d) => {
              const v = parseFloat(el.getAttribute(k));
              return isFinite(v) ? v : d;
            };
      
            if (tag === 'circle') {
              // A circle stays a circle only under a similarity transform; a squashed one
              // becomes four cubic pieces.
              const cx = num('cx', 0), cy = num('cy', 0), r = num('r', 0);
              if (r > 0) {
                if (matIsSimilarity(m)) {
                  ctx.items.push({
                    kind: 'circle', c: matApply(m, { x: cx, y: cy }), r: r * Math.hypot(m[0], m[1]),
                    color, filled, group, clip, source: tag
                  });
                } else {
                  const flat = svgSegmentsToSubpaths(svgEllipseSegs(cx, cy, r, r), m);
                  if (flat.length) ctx.items.push({ kind: 'paths', subpaths: flat, color, filled, group, clip, source: tag });
                }
                ctx.colors.set(color, (ctx.colors.get(color) || 0) + 1);
              }
              return;
            }
      
            const subs = svgSegmentsToSubpaths(svgShapeSegs(el), m);
            if (subs.length) {
              ctx.items.push({ kind: 'paths', subpaths: subs, color, filled, group, clip, source: tag });
              ctx.colors.set(color, (ctx.colors.get(color) || 0) + 1);
            }
            return;
          }
      
          if (tag === 'text') { ctx.skipped.text++; return; }
          if (tag === 'image') { ctx.skipped.image++; return; }
          if (tag === 'use') {
            const id = svgHref(el);
            const target = id && ctx.byId.get(id);
            if (target && ctx.useDepth < 12) {
              ctx.useDepth++;
              svgWalk(target, ctx, m, style, group, depth + 1);
              ctx.useDepth--;
            } else if (!target) ctx.skipped.use++;
            return;
          }
      
          if (tag === 'defs' || tag === 'symbol' || tag === 'clippath' || tag === 'marker' ||
            tag === 'mask' || tag === 'pattern' || tag === 'lineargradient' || tag === 'radialgradient') {
            return;                                  // only reachable through <use>
          }
      
          const kids = el.childNodes || [];
          for (let i = 0; i < kids.length; i++) {

        // clip-path="url(#id)": the clip outline is collected in world space, the caller
        // intersects it with Clipper.
        function svgResolveClip(attr, ctx, ctm, owner) {
          const m = String(attr).match(/url\(\s*['"]?#([^)'"]+)/);
          if (!m) return null;
          const node = ctx.byId.get(m[1]);
          if (!node) return null;
          const units = (node.getAttribute('clipPathUnits') || 'userSpaceOnUse').trim();
          let base = ctm;
          if (units === 'objectBoundingBox') {
            const b = svgOwnBBox(owner);
            if (!b) return null;
            base = matMul(ctm, [b.w, 0, 0, b.h, b.x, b.y]);
          }
          const subpaths = [];
          const collect = (el, ctm2, depth) => {
            if (!el || depth > 12) return;
            const tag = String(el.nodeName || '').toLowerCase();
            if (SVG_SHAPES[tag]) {
              const m2 = matMul(ctm2, parseTransformList(el.getAttribute && el.getAttribute('transform')));
              for (const sub of svgSegmentsToSubpaths(svgShapeSegs(el), m2)) subpaths.push(sub);
              return;
            }
            const kids = el.childNodes || [];
            for (let i = 0; i < kids.length; i++) {
              const kid = kids[i];
              if (kid.nodeType === 3) continue;
              const t = String(kid.nodeName || '').toLowerCase();
              if (t === 'defs') continue;
              const m3 = matMul(ctm2, parseTransformList(kid.getAttribute && kid.getAttribute('transform')));
              if (SVG_SHAPES[t]) {
                for (const sub of svgSegmentsToSubpaths(svgShapeSegs(kid), m3)) subpaths.push(sub);
              } else collect(kid, ctm2, depth + 1);
            }
          };
          collect(node, base, 0);
          return subpaths.length ? subpaths : null;
        }
      
        function svgOwnBBox(el) {
          const tag = String(el.nodeName || '').toLowerCase();
          const num = (k, d) => {
            const v = parseFloat(el.getAttribute(k));
            return isFinite(v) ? v : d;
          };
          if (tag === 'rect') return { x: num('x', 0), y: num('y', 0), w: num('width', 0), h: num('height', 0) };
          if (tag === 'circle') { const r = num('r', 0); return { x: num('cx', 0) - r, y: num('cy', 0) - r, w: 2 * r, h: 2 * r }; }
          if (tag === 'ellipse') {
            const rx = num('rx', 0), ry = num('ry', 0);
            return { x: num('cx', 0) - rx, y: num('cy', 0) - ry, w: 2 * rx, h: 2 * ry };
          }
          return null;
        }
      
        // Flatten a subpath to a polygon (for clipping / measurement).
        function svgSubpathPolygon(sub, steps) {
          const n = Math.max(6, steps || 10);
          const out = [];
          const nodes = sub.nodes;
          for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
            const a = nodes[i];
            const b = nodes[(i + 1) % nodes.length];
            if (!(sub.closed) && i === nodes.length - 1) break;
            out.push({ x: a.x, y: a.y });
            if (a.o || b.i) {
              const c1 = a.o || a;
              const c2 = b.i || b;
              for (let s = 1; s < n; s++) {
                const t = s / n, u = 1 - t;
                out.push({
                  x: u * u * u * a.x + 3 * u * u * t * c1.x + 3 * u * t * t * c2.x + t * t * t * b.x,
                  y: u * u * u * a.y + 3 * u * u * t * c1.y + 3 * u * t * t * c2.y + t * t * t * b.y
                });
              }
            }
          }
          return out;
