        // Real clip: intersection of the shape and its clip outline. Runs on the
        // exact engine (PathBool) — the input subpaths are flattened polygons, but
        // the operation itself keeps full float precision instead of Clipper's
        // 0.001 mm integer grid.
        function svgClipSubpaths(subpaths, clipSubpaths) {
          const PB = pathBoolLib();
          if (!PB) return null;
          const toPath = (subs) => subs
            .filter(s => s.closed && s.nodes.length >= 3)
            .map(s => {
              // curves flatten first (same as the Clipper version): the clip itself
              // runs on polygons, at full float precision
              const pts = svgSubpathPolygon(s, 12);
              if (pts.length < 3) return null;
              return [...PB.pathFromCommands([
                ['M', [pts[0].x, pts[0].y]],
                ...pts.slice(1).map(p => ['L', [p.x, p.y]]),
                ['Z']
              ])];
            })
            .filter(Boolean);
          const subject = toPath(subpaths);
          const clipPaths = toPath(clipSubpaths);
          if (!subject.length || !clipPaths.length) return [];
          // all subject rings travel as ONE path (union by NonZero), all clip rings
          // as the other — the same subject ∩ clip semantics as the Clipper version
          const result = (() => {
            try {
              return new PB.PathBoolean([
                { path: subject.flat(), fillRule: PB.FillRule.NonZero },
                { path: clipPaths.flat(), fillRule: PB.FillRule.NonZero }
              ]).get(PB.PathBooleanOperation.Intersection);
            } catch (err) {
              console.warn('PathBool clip failed:', err);
              return null;
            }
          })();
          if (!result) return null;
          // split the result back into subpaths and hand them out as polyline nodes
          const out = [];
          for (const path of result) {
            let cur = null;
            for (const seg of path) {
              const st = seg[1];
              const en = seg[0] === 'L' ? seg[2]
                : seg[0] === 'C' ? seg[4]
                : seg[0] === 'Q' ? seg[3] : seg[7];
              if (!cur || Math.hypot(cur[cur.length - 1].x - st[0], cur[cur.length - 1].y - st[1]) > 1e-6) {
                if (cur && cur.length >= 3) out.push({ nodes: cur, closed: true, curved: false });
                cur = [{ x: st[0], y: st[1], i: null, o: null }];
              }
              cur.push({ x: en[0], y: en[1], i: null, o: null });
            }
            if (cur && cur.length >= 3) out.push({ nodes: cur, closed: true, curved: false });
          }
          return out;
        }
      
        // --- document level ----------------------------------------------------------
        const SVG_DEFAULT_STYLE = {
          stroke: 'none', fill: '#000000', fillSet: false, strokeWidth: '1', fillRule: 'nonzero',
          display: null, visibility: null, opacity: null
        };
      
        function svgCollectBounds(items) {
          let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
          const put = (x, y) => {
            if (!isFinite(x) || !isFinite(y)) return;
            if (x < minX) minX = x;
            if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
            if (y < minY) minY = y;
            if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
          };
          for (const item of items) {
            if (item.kind === 'circle') {
              put(item.c.x - item.r, item.c.y - item.r);
              put(item.c.x + item.r, item.c.y + item.r);
            } else {
              for (const sub of (item.subpaths || [])) {
                for (const n of sub.nodes) {
                  put(n.x, n.y);
                  if (n.i) put(n.i.x, n.i.y);
                  if (n.o) put(n.o.x, n.o.y);
                }
              }
            }
          }
          if (!isFinite(minX)) return null;
          return { minX, minY, maxX, maxY, w: maxX - minX, h: maxY - minY };
        }
      
        // Parse an SVG string into world-space items (no document changes yet).
        function svgImportParse(text, opts) {
          const o = opts || {};
          const parser = new DOMParser();
          const doc = parser.parseFromString(String(text || ''), 'image/svg+xml');
          const err = doc.getElementsByTagName('parsererror');
          if (err && err.length) throw new Error('SVG could not be parsed');
          const root = doc.documentElement;
          if (!root || String(root.nodeName).toLowerCase() !== 'svg') throw new Error('Not an SVG document');
      
          const byId = new Map();
          const all = root.getElementsByTagName('*');
          for (let i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
            const id = svgLocalId(all[i]);
            if (id && !byId.has(id)) byId.set(id, all[i]);
          }
          const styleNodes = root.getElementsByTagName('style');
          let rules = [];
          for (let i = 0; i < styleNodes.length; i++) {
            rules = rules.concat(svgParseStyleSheets(styleNodes[i].textContent || ''));
          }
      
          // user units -> mm, and flip Y so the sketch stays Y-up
          const vbRaw = String(root.getAttribute('viewBox') || '').trim();
          const vb = vbRaw ? vbRaw.split(/[\s,]+/).map(Number) : null;
          const wMm = svgLenToMm(root.getAttribute('width'));
          const hMm = svgLenToMm(root.getAttribute('height'));
          let scale = 1;
          if (vb && vb.length === 4 && vb[2] > 0 && vb[3] > 0) {
            if (wMm > 0) scale = wMm / vb[2];
            else if (hMm > 0) scale = hMm / vb[3];
          } else if (wMm > 0 && !vb) {
            scale = 1;                                // already mm through the width unit
          }
          if (isFinite(o.scale) && o.scale > 0) scale *= o.scale;
          const vbX = vb && isFinite(vb[0]) ? vb[0] : 0;
          const vbY = vb && isFinite(vb[1]) ? vb[1] : 0;
          // Default flip: file SVG is Y-down (screen convention), the sketch is Y-up.
          // o.flipY === false reads the SVG AS Y-up already (AI-authored drawings):
          // y' = scale*(y - vbY), so the model writes coordinates the way it thinks
          // — bottom-left origin, up is +Y — and nothing arrives mirrored.
          const base = o.flipY === false
            ? [scale, 0, 0, scale, -vbX * scale, -vbY * scale]
            : [scale, 0, 0, -scale, -vbX * scale, vbY * scale];
      
          const ctx = {
            rules,
            byId,
            items: [],
            colors: new Map(),
            skipped: { text: 0, image: 0, use: 0, clip: 0 },
            groupSeq: 0,
            groupNames: {},
            useDepth: 0,
            prefix: o.prefix || 'imp'
          };
      
          if (String(root.getAttribute('display') || '') === 'none' ||
            String(root.getAttribute('visibility') || '') === 'hidden') {
            throw new Error('SVG is hidden');
          }
      
          svgWalk(root, ctx, base, SVG_DEFAULT_STYLE, null, 0);
      
          let segments = 0;
          for (const item of ctx.items) {
            if (item.kind === 'circle') { segments += 4; continue; }
            for (const sub of (item.subpaths || [])) segments += Math.max(1, sub.nodes.length);
          }
          const bounds = svgCollectBounds(ctx.items);
          const colors = Array.from(ctx.colors.entries()).map(([hex, count]) => ({ hex, count }))
            .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count);
          return {
            items: ctx.items,
            bounds: bounds,
            scale: scale,
            mmW: bounds ? bounds.w : 0,
            mmH: bounds ? bounds.h : 0,
            colorList: colors,
            segments: segments,
            skipped: ctx.skipped,
            groupNames: ctx.groupNames,
            groupCount: Object.keys(ctx.groupNames).length
          };
        }
      
        // Insert the parsed items into the sketch. One undo step, called by the dialog.
        function svgImportInsert(ir, opts) {
          const o = opts || {};
          const fillMode = o.fillMode || 'gray';
          const colorMap = o.colorMap || {};
          const keepGroups = !!o.keepGroups;
          const target = o.center || s2w(W / 2, H / 2);
          const bb = ir.bounds || { minX: 0, minY: 0, maxX: 0, maxY: 0 };
          const off = { x: target.x - (bb.minX + bb.maxX) / 2, y: target.y - (bb.minY + bb.maxY) / 2 };
      
          const cache = new Map();
          const mkPoint = (x, y) => {
            const key = Math.round(x * 1e6) + ',' + Math.round(y * 1e6);
            if (cache.has(key)) return cache.get(key);
            const id = privatePoint(x, y);
            cache.set(key, id);
            return id;
          };
          const colorOfSrc = (hex) => colorMap[hex] || hex;
          const made = { entities: [], groups: new Set(), lines: 0, splines: 0, circles: 0, framed: 0 };
          const groupBag = new Map();
      
          const tagEntity = (ent, item) => {
            if (item.group) {
              ent.group = item.group;
              made.groups.add(item.group);
            }
            return ent;
          };
      
          const addSubpaths = (subs, item, forceFlat) => {
            let firstId = null;
            for (const sub of subs) {
              if (!sub.nodes || sub.nodes.length < 2) continue;
              // A path that stops within the join tolerance of where it began is closed,
              // whichever way the exporter wrote it (Z, or just a hairline gap).
              const subFirst = sub.nodes[0];
              const subLast = sub.nodes[sub.nodes.length - 1];
              const nearClosed = !sub.closed && sub.nodes.length > 2 &&
                Math.hypot(subFirst.x - subLast.x, subFirst.y - subLast.y) < JOIN_TOL;
              const isClosed = !!sub.closed || nearClosed;
              if (sub.curved && !forceFlat) {
                const pids = sub.nodes.map(n => mkPoint(n.x + off.x, n.y + off.y));
                if (new Set(pids).size < 2) continue;
                const hs = { o: [], i: [] };
                for (const n of sub.nodes) {
                  hs.o.push(n.o ? { x: n.o.x + off.x, y: n.o.y + off.y } : null);
                  hs.i.push(n.i ? { x: n.i.x + off.x, y: n.i.y + off.y } : null);
                }
                const id = newId();
                state.entities[id] = tagEntity({
                  id, type: 'spline', pts: pids, hs, closed: isClosed,
                  color: item.color, layer: ui.activeLayer
                }, item);
                if (!firstId) firstId = id;
                made.entities.push(id);
                made.splines++;
                continue;
              }
              const pids = sub.nodes.map(n => mkPoint(n.x + off.x, n.y + off.y));
              const chain = isClosed ? pids.concat([pids[0]]) : pids;
              for (let i = 1; i < chain.length; i++) {
                if (chain[i - 1] === chain[i]) continue;
                const id = newId();
                state.entities[id] = tagEntity({
                  id, type: 'line', a: chain[i - 1], b: chain[i],
                  color: item.color, layer: ui.activeLayer
                }, item);
                if (!firstId) firstId = id;
                made.entities.push(id);
                made.lines++;
              }
            }
            if (firstId && item.filled && fillMode !== 'ignore') state.entities[firstId].fill = fillMode;
            return firstId;
          };
      
          for (const item of ir.items) {
            const color = colorOfSrc(item.color);
            const meta = { color, filled: item.filled, group: keepGroups ? null : item.group };
      
            if (item.kind === 'circle') {
              const cid = mkPoint(item.c.x + off.x, item.c.y + off.y);
              const id = newId();
              state.entities[id] = tagEntity({
                id, type: 'circle', c: cid, r: item.r, color, layer: ui.activeLayer
              }, meta);
              if (item.filled && fillMode !== 'ignore') state.entities[id].fill = fillMode;
              made.entities.push(id);
              made.circles++;
              continue;
            }
      
            let subs = item.subpaths || [];
            if (item.clip && subs.length) {
              const clipped = svgClipSubpaths(subs, item.clip);
              if (clipped) subs = clipped; else ir.skipped.clip++;
            }
            if (!subs.length) continue;
      
            if (keepGroups && item.group) {
              const bag = groupBag.get(item.group) || { color, filled: false, contours: [], curves: [] };
              bag.filled = bag.filled || item.filled;
              for (const sub of subs) {
                const pts = svgSubpathPolygon(sub, 12).map(p => ({ x: p.x + off.x, y: p.y + off.y }));
                if (pts.length >= 2) bag.contours.push({ pts, closed: !!sub.closed });
                // keep the real curve data (or the exact polyline) of every subpath,
                // so Ctrl+Shift+G can rebuild the original shapes instead of a flat
                // approximation of them
                bag.curves.push(sub.curved ? {
                  closed: !!sub.closed,
                  curved: true,
                  nodes: sub.nodes.map(n => ({
                    x: n.x + off.x, y: n.y + off.y,
                    o: n.o ? { x: n.o.x + off.x, y: n.o.y + off.y } : null,
                    i: n.i ? { x: n.i.x + off.x, y: n.i.y + off.y } : null
                  }))
                } : {
                  closed: !!sub.closed,
                  curved: false,
                  nodes: sub.nodes.map(n => ({ x: n.x + off.x, y: n.y + off.y, o: null, i: null }))
                });
              }
              groupBag.set(item.group, bag);
              continue;
            }
      
            addSubpaths(subs, meta, false);
          }
      
          for (const [group, bag] of groupBag) {
            if (!bag.contours.length) continue;
            const id = addFramedObject(bag.contours, {
              color: bag.color,
              layer: ui.activeLayer,
              name: ir.groupNames[group] || '',
              tol: ART_TOL_MM
            });
            if (id != null) {
              const ent = state.entities[id];
              if (ent) {
                ent.group = group;
                if (bag.filled && fillMode !== 'ignore') ent.fill = fillMode;
              }
              made.entities.push(id);
              made.groups.add(group);
              made.framed++;
            }
          }
      
          return made;
        }
      
        // --- dialog -------------------------------------------------------------------
        let svgImportJob = null;
      
        function openSvgImport(file) {
          file.text().then((text) => {
            let ir = null;
            try {
              ir = svgImportParse(text, { prefix: 'i' + Date.now().toString(36) + '-' });
            } catch (err) {
              toast('That SVG could not be read (' + (err && err.message ? err.message : 'parse error') + ')');
              return;
            }
            if (!ir.items.length) {
              const bits = [];
              if (ir.skipped.text) bits.push(ir.skipped.text + ' text');
              if (ir.skipped.image) bits.push(ir.skipped.image + ' image');
              toast('No vector shapes found' + (bits.length ? ' (skipped ' + bits.join(', ') + ')' : ''));
              return;
            }
            svgImportJob = { name: file.name, ir: ir };
            buildSvgImportDialog();
            const modal = document.getElementById('svg-import-modal');
            if (modal) modal.classList.remove('hidden');
          }).catch(() => toast('Could not read that file'));
        }
      
        function closeSvgImport() {
          const modal = document.getElementById('svg-import-modal');
          if (modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
          svgImportJob = null;
        }
      
        function buildSvgImportDialog() {
          const job = svgImportJob;
          if (!job) return;
          const ir = job.ir;
          const nameEl = document.getElementById('imp-file');
          if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = job.name;
      
          const bits = [];
          bits.push(fmt(ir.mmW) + ' × ' + fmt(ir.mmH) + ' mm');
          bits.push(ir.items.length + (ir.items.length === 1 ? ' shape' : ' shapes'));
          if (ir.segments) bits.push(ir.segments + ' segments');
          if (ir.groupCount) bits.push(ir.groupCount + (ir.groupCount === 1 ? ' group' : ' groups'));
          if (ir.skipped.text) bits.push(ir.skipped.text + ' text skipped');
          if (ir.skipped.image) bits.push(ir.skipped.image + ' image skipped');
          if (ir.skipped.use) bits.push(ir.skipped.use + ' unresolved use');
          const sumEl = document.getElementById('imp-summary');
          if (sumEl) sumEl.textContent = bits.join(' · ');
          const warnEl = document.getElementById('imp-warn');
          if (warnEl) {
            const heavy = ir.segments > 8000;
            warnEl.hidden = !heavy;
            warnEl.textContent = heavy
              ? 'Large file: ' + ir.segments + ' segments. The window will freeze for a few seconds while it is built — turn "keep each group as one object" on to make it much lighter.'
              : '';
          }
      
          const host = document.getElementById('imp-colors');
          if (host) {
            host.innerHTML = '';
            for (const entry of ir.colorList) {
              const row = document.createElement('label');
              row.className = 'imp-color';
              const sw = document.createElement('i');
              sw.className = 'imp-swatch';
              sw.style.background = entry.hex;
              const code = document.createElement('code');
              code.textContent = entry.hex;
              const count = document.createElement('span');
              count.className = 'imp-count';
              count.textContent = entry.count + '×';
              const sel = document.createElement('select');
              sel.dataset.src = entry.hex;
              const picked = nearestCncColor(entry.hex);
              for (const c of COLORS) {
                const opt = document.createElement('option');
                opt.value = c.value;
                opt.textContent = c.name;
                if (c.value === picked) opt.selected = true;
                sel.appendChild(opt);
              }
              row.appendChild(sw);
              row.appendChild(code);
              row.appendChild(count);
              row.appendChild(sel);
              host.appendChild(row);
            }
            if (!ir.colorList.length) {
              const none = document.createElement('div');
              none.className = 'imp-count';
              none.textContent = 'No stroke or fill colours — everything comes in as cut (black).';
              host.appendChild(none);
            }
          }
        }
      
        function runSvgImport() {
          const job = svgImportJob;
          if (!job) return;
          const keepGroups = !!(document.getElementById('imp-keep-groups') || {}).checked;
          const fillEl = document.getElementById('imp-fill');
          const fillMode = fillEl ? fillEl.value : 'gray';
          const colorMap = {};
          for (const sel of document.querySelectorAll('#imp-colors select[data-src]')) {
            colorMap[sel.dataset.src] = sel.value;
          }
          const wasEmpty = !Object.keys(state.entities).length;
          pushHistory();
          let made = null;
          try {
            made = svgImportInsert(job.ir, { keepGroups, fillMode, colorMap });
          } catch (err) {
            history.pop();
            updateHistoryButtons();
            toast('Import failed: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : 'unknown error'));
            return;
          }
          ui.selection = made.entities.map(id => ({ kind: 'entity', id }));
          invalidateLoops();
          commit();
          if (wasEmpty) fitView();
          const parts = [];
          if (made.lines) parts.push(made.lines + ' line' + (made.lines === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          if (made.splines) parts.push(made.splines + ' curve' + (made.splines === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          if (made.circles) parts.push(made.circles + ' circle' + (made.circles === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          if (made.framed) parts.push(made.framed + ' framed object' + (made.framed === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          const skipped = job.ir.skipped;
          const sk = [];
          if (skipped.text) sk.push(skipped.text + ' text');
          if (skipped.image) sk.push(skipped.image + ' image');
          if (skipped.clip) sk.push(skipped.clip + ' clip could not be cut');
          toast('Imported ' + (parts.length ? parts.join(' · ') : 'nothing') +
            (made.groups.size ? ' · ' + made.groups.size + ' group' + (made.groups.size === 1 ? '' : 's') : '') +
            (sk.length ? ' · skipped ' + sk.join(', ') : ''));
          closeSvgImport();
        }

          { group: 'Renderer', key: 'pointZoomMax', label: 'Point max at zoom', unit: '%',

        // MiniSketch application shell. Pure math/geometry lives in core.js (loaded
        // first, see ARCHITECTURE.md for the data model and the invariants that must

        // this file only through the toolApi() surface. Rough map, top to bottom:
        //
        //   constants + colour codes      document model (blankState, P, entityPointIds)
        //   SVG import analysis           layer helpers + pointUses index
        //   settings (SETTINGS_SPEC)      view / camera (w2s, s2w, fitView)
        //   theme                         history + persistence + serializeState
        //   UI shell (blankState/ui)      loops (getLoops, invalidateLoops)
        //   solver wiring (solveSketch)   constraint conflicts + commands (cmd*)
        //   transform mode (xf*)          copy / paste
        //   dimension geometry            drawing (render, draw*)
        //   hit-testing                   panels / menus / keyboard
        //   KaryaCNC send                 SVG import insert / export

        // breaking them: never scan all entities per point (use pointUses), and the

        const VIEW_KEY = 'minisketch.v1.view';
        const LAYOUT_KEY = 'minisketch.v1.layout';

          { name: 'Cut (black)', value: '#000000' },
          { name: 'Engrave (blue)', value: '#0000ff' },
          { name: 'V-carve (cyan)', value: '#00ffff' },
          { name: 'Pocket (purple)', value: '#800080' },
          { name: 'Drill (red)', value: '#ff0000' },
          { name: 'Mark (green)', value: '#00ff00' },
          { name: 'Cut tab (maroon)', value: '#800000' },
          { name: 'Start point (teal)', value: '#008080' }
        ];
      
        // the hex codes on their own, for the import colour mapping

        const CANVAS_DARK = Object.create(null);

        }
        let themeName = 'dark';
      
        function themedCanvasColor(v) {
          if (themeName !== 'dark') return v;
          return CANVAS_DARK[v.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '')] || v;
        }
      
        function themeCanvas() {
          const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(ctx);
          for (const prop of ['strokeStyle', 'fillStyle']) {
            const desc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(proto, prop);
            if (!desc || !desc.set) continue;
            let current;
            Object.defineProperty(ctx, prop, {
              configurable: true,
              get() { return current; },
              set(v) {
                current = typeof v === 'string' ? themedCanvasColor(v) : v;

          if (!opts || opts.persist !== false) {
            try { localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, themeName); } catch (err) { /* ignore */ }
          }
          const btn = document.getElementById('btn-theme');
          if (btn) {
            btn.textContent = themeName === 'dark' ? '\u2600' : '\u263e';

            btn.setAttribute('aria-label', btn.title);

        function toggleTheme() {
          const next = themeName === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
          applyTheme(next);
          try {
            if (window.parent && window.parent !== window)
              window.parent.postMessage({ type: 'karyacnc-set-theme', theme: next }, '*');
          } catch (err) { /* ignore */ }
        }
      
      
        // ---- Import: SVG ----------------------------------------------------------
        // The model has no nested transforms, so an imported tree is flattened: the
        // group chain is composed into world coordinates while walking. Structure is
        // kept cheaply instead: layers, a flat group tag, or one framed object per
        // group ("keep each group as one object").
        const SVG_MM_PER = { mm: 1, cm: 10, m: 1000, in: 25.4, pt: 25.4 / 72, pc: 25.4 / 6, px: 25.4 / 96, q: 0.25 };
      
        function svgLenToMm(raw) {
          if (raw == null) return null;
          const m = String(raw).trim().match(/^(-?[\d.]+(?:e[-+]?\d+)?)\s*([a-z%]*)$/i);
          if (!m) return null;
          const n = parseFloat(m[1]);
          if (!isFinite(n)) return null;
          const unit = (m[2] || 'px').toLowerCase();
          if (unit === '%') return null;
          const k = SVG_MM_PER[unit];
          return k == null ? null : n * k;
        }
      
        // --- colour -----------------------------------------------------------------
        const SVG_NAMED = {
          black: '#000000', white: '#ffffff', red: '#ff0000', green: '#008000', lime: '#00ff00',
          blue: '#0000ff', yellow: '#ffff00', cyan: '#00ffff', aqua: '#00ffff', magenta: '#ff00ff',
          fuchsia: '#ff00ff', gray: '#808080', grey: '#808080', silver: '#c0c0c0', maroon: '#800000',
          olive: '#808000', navy: '#000080', purple: '#800080', teal: '#008080', orange: '#ffa500'
        };
      
        function svgColorToHex(str) {
          if (str == null) return null;
          const v = String(str).trim().toLowerCase();
          if (!v || v === 'none' || v === 'transparent') return null;
          if (v[0] === '#') {
            const h = v.slice(1);
            if (h.length === 3) return '#' + h[0] + h[0] + h[1] + h[1] + h[2] + h[2];
            if (h.length === 6) return '#' + h;
            return null;
          }
          const m = v.match(/^rgba?\(([^)]+)\)$/);
          if (m) {
            const parts = m[1].split(/[,/\s]+/).filter(Boolean).map(parseFloat);
            if (parts.length >= 3) {
              if (parts.length > 3 && parts[3] === 0) return null;
              const hex = parts.slice(0, 3).map(n => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(n))).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
              return '#' + hex;
            }
          }
          if (SVG_NAMED[v]) return SVG_NAMED[v];
          if (/^url\(/.test(v)) return null;                       // gradient / pattern
          return null;

        function hexToRgb(hex) {
          const h = String(hex || '').replace('#', '');
          if (h.length !== 6) return null;
          return [parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16), parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16), parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16)];
        }
      
        // sRGB -> CIE Lab (D65), so "nearest" matches the eye and not the raw numbers.
        function rgbToLab(r, g, b) {
          const lin = (c) => {
            const s = c / 255;
            return s <= 0.04045 ? s / 12.92 : Math.pow((s + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
          };
          const R = lin(r), G = lin(g), B = lin(b);
          const X = (R * 0.4124 + G * 0.3576 + B * 0.1805) / 0.95047;
          const Y = (R * 0.2126 + G * 0.7152 + B * 0.0722) / 1.0;
          const Z = (R * 0.0193 + G * 0.1192 + B * 0.9505) / 1.08883;
          const f = (t) => (t > 0.008856 ? Math.cbrt(t) : (7.787 * t + 16 / 116));
          return [116 * f(Y) - 16, 500 * (f(X) - f(Y)), 200 * (f(Y) - f(Z))];
        }
      
        function colorDistanceLab(a, b) {
          const A = rgbToLab(a[0], a[1], a[2]);
          const B = rgbToLab(b[0], b[1], b[2]);
          return Math.hypot(A[0] - B[0], A[1] - B[1], A[2] - B[2]);
        }
      
        // Nearest KaryaCNC stroke code; ties fall back to black (cut).
        function nearestCncColor(hex) {
          const rgb = hexToRgb(hex);
          if (!rgb) return '#000000';
          let best = '#000000';
          let bestD = Infinity;
          for (const code of CNC_CODES) {
            const c = hexToRgb(code);
            if (!c) continue;
            const d = colorDistanceLab(rgb, c);
            if (d < bestD - 1e-9) { bestD = d; best = code; }
          }
          return best;
        }
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
        // --- style -------------------------------------------------------------------
        function svgStyleDecls(text) {
          const out = {};
          for (const part of String(text || '').split(';')) {
            const at = part.indexOf(':');
            if (at < 0) continue;
            const k = part.slice(0, at).trim().toLowerCase();
            const v = part.slice(at + 1).trim();
            if (k) out[k] = v;

        // <style> blocks: only simple ".class { }" / "tag { }" rules are honoured.
        function svgParseStyleSheets(text) {
          const rules = [];
          const re = /([^{}]+)\{([^}]*)\}/g;
          let m;
          while ((m = re.exec(text))) {
            const sel = m[1].trim();
            const decls = svgStyleDecls(m[2]);
            if (!Object.keys(decls).length) continue;
            rules.push({ sel, decls });
          }
          return rules;

        function svgApplyRules(rules, el, style) {
          const tag = String(el.nodeName || '').toLowerCase();
          const cls = String(el.getAttribute ? (el.getAttribute('class') || '') : '').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
          const id = el.getAttribute ? (el.getAttribute('id') || '') : '';
          for (const rule of rules) {
            for (const one of rule.sel.split(',')) {
              const s = one.trim();
              if (!s) continue;
              let hit = false;
              if (s[0] === '.' && cls.indexOf(s.slice(1)) >= 0) hit = true;
              else if (s[0] === '#' && id && s.slice(1) === id) hit = true;
              else if (/^[a-z]+$/i.test(s) && s.toLowerCase() === tag) hit = true;
              if (hit) {
                const k = rule.decls;
                if (k.stroke != null) style.stroke = k.stroke;
                if (k.fill != null) { style.fill = k.fill; style.fillSet = true; }
                if (k['stroke-width'] != null) style.strokeWidth = k['stroke-width'];
                if (k['fill-rule'] != null) style.fillRule = k['fill-rule'];
                if (k.display != null) style.display = k.display;
                if (k.visibility != null) style.visibility = k.visibility;
                if (k.opacity != null) style.opacity = k.opacity;

        function svgStyleFor(el, parent, rules) {

            strokeWidth: parent.strokeWidth, fillRule: parent.fillRule,
            display: parent.display, visibility: parent.visibility, opacity: parent.opacity
          };
          const attr = (k) => (el.getAttribute && el.hasAttribute && el.hasAttribute(k) ? el.getAttribute(k) : null);
          const keys = {
            stroke: 'stroke', fill: 'fill', 'stroke-width': 'strokeWidth',
            'fill-rule': 'fillRule', display: 'display', visibility: 'visibility', opacity: 'opacity'
          };
          for (const k in keys) {
            const v = attr(k);
            if (v != null) {

            if (d.fill != null) st.fill = d.fill;
            if (d.fill != null) st.fillSet = true;
            if (d['stroke-width'] != null) st.strokeWidth = d['stroke-width'];
            if (d['fill-rule'] != null) st.fillRule = d['fill-rule'];
            if (d.display != null) st.display = d.display;
            if (d.visibility != null) st.visibility = d.visibility;
            if (d.opacity != null) st.opacity = d.opacity;

        function svgHidden(style) {

          return false;
        }
      
        // --- tree walker -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Everything lands in ctx.items as { subpaths, color, filled, group, clip } with
        // world coordinates already baked.
        function svgLocalId(el) {
          return el && el.getAttribute ? (el.getAttribute('id') || '') : '';

        function svgHref(el) {
          const raw = (el.getAttribute && (el.getAttribute('href') || el.getAttribute('xlink:href'))) || '';

        }
      
        function svgPointsAttr(raw) {
          const nums = String(raw || '').match(/-?[\d.]+(?:e[-+]?\d+)?/g);
          if (!nums) return [];
          const pts = [];
          for (let i = 0; i + 1 < nums.length; i += 2) pts.push({ x: +nums[i], y: +nums[i + 1] });
          return pts;
        }
      
        function svgRoundRectSegs(x, y, w, h, rx, ry) {

          if (!(w > 0) || !(h > 0)) return segs;
          rx = Math.max(0, Math.min(rx == null ? 0 : rx, w / 2));
          ry = Math.max(0, Math.min(ry == null ? rx : ry, h / 2));
          segs.push({ t: 'M', p: { x: x + rx, y } });
          if (rx > 0 && ry > 0) {
            segs.push({ t: 'L', p: { x: x + w - rx, y } });
            segs.push(...svgArcToCubics(x + w - rx, y, rx, ry, 0, false, true, x + w, y + ry));
            segs.push({ t: 'L', p: { x: x + w, y: y + h - ry } });
            segs.push(...svgArcToCubics(x + w, y + h - ry, rx, ry, 0, false, true, x + w - rx, y + h));
            segs.push({ t: 'L', p: { x: x + rx, y: y + h } });
            segs.push(...svgArcToCubics(x + rx, y + h, rx, ry, 0, false, true, x, y + h - ry));
            segs.push({ t: 'L', p: { x, y: y + ry } });
            segs.push(...svgArcToCubics(x, y + ry, rx, ry, 0, false, true, x + rx, y));
          } else {
            segs.push({ t: 'L', p: { x: x + w, y } }, { t: 'L', p: { x: x + w, y: y + h } },

          return segs;

        function svgEllipseSegs(cx, cy, rx, ry) {

            { t: 'M', p: { x: cx + rx, y: cy } },
            { t: 'C', from: { x: cx + rx, y: cy }, c1: { x: cx + rx, y: cy + oy }, c2: { x: cx + ox, y: cy + ry }, p: { x: cx, y: cy + ry } },
            { t: 'C', from: { x: cx, y: cy + ry }, c1: { x: cx - ox, y: cy + ry }, c2: { x: cx - rx, y: cy + oy }, p: { x: cx - rx, y: cy } },
            { t: 'C', from: { x: cx - rx, y: cy }, c1: { x: cx - rx, y: cy - oy }, c2: { x: cx - ox, y: cy - ry }, p: { x: cx, y: cy - ry } },
            { t: 'C', from: { x: cx, y: cy - ry }, c1: { x: cx + ox, y: cy - ry }, c2: { x: cx + rx, y: cy - oy }, p: { x: cx + rx, y: cy } },

        const SVG_SHAPES = { path: 1, polyline: 1, polygon: 1, line: 1, rect: 1, circle: 1, ellipse: 1 };

          if (svgHidden(style)) return;

          let group = groupTag;
          if (tag === 'g' && !group) {
            ctx.groupSeq++;

          const clipAttr = el.getAttribute && el.getAttribute('clip-path');

          if (SVG_SHAPES[tag]) {

            const fillHex = svgColorToHex(style.fill);
            const filled = !!fillHex && style.fillSet === true;
            const color = strokeHex || fillHex || '#000000';
            const num = (k, d) => {

            };
      
            if (tag === 'circle') {

              // becomes four cubic pieces.
              const cx = num('cx', 0), cy = num('cy', 0), r = num('r', 0);
              if (r > 0) {
                if (matIsSimilarity(m)) {
                  ctx.items.push({

                  });
                } else {
                  const flat = svgSegmentsToSubpaths(svgEllipseSegs(cx, cy, r, r), m);
                  if (flat.length) ctx.items.push({ kind: 'paths', subpaths: flat, color, filled, group, clip, source: tag });

                ctx.colors.set(color, (ctx.colors.get(color) || 0) + 1);

            const subs = svgSegmentsToSubpaths(svgShapeSegs(el), m);
            if (subs.length) {
              ctx.items.push({ kind: 'paths', subpaths: subs, color, filled, group, clip, source: tag });

            return;

          if (tag === 'text') { ctx.skipped.text++; return; }
          if (tag === 'image') { ctx.skipped.image++; return; }

          if (tag === 'defs' || tag === 'symbol' || tag === 'clippath' || tag === 'marker' ||

        // clip-path="url(#id)": the clip outline is collected in world space, the caller

          if (!m) return null;

          if (!node) return null;
          const units = (node.getAttribute('clipPathUnits') || 'userSpaceOnUse').trim();

          const subpaths = [];
          const collect = (el, ctm2, depth) => {
            if (!el || depth > 12) return;
            const tag = String(el.nodeName || '').toLowerCase();
            if (SVG_SHAPES[tag]) {
              const m2 = matMul(ctm2, parseTransformList(el.getAttribute && el.getAttribute('transform')));
              for (const sub of svgSegmentsToSubpaths(svgShapeSegs(el), m2)) subpaths.push(sub);

            }
            const kids = el.childNodes || [];
            for (let i = 0; i < kids.length; i++) {
              const kid = kids[i];
              if (kid.nodeType === 3) continue;
              const t = String(kid.nodeName || '').toLowerCase();
              if (t === 'defs') continue;
              const m3 = matMul(ctm2, parseTransformList(kid.getAttribute && kid.getAttribute('transform')));
              if (SVG_SHAPES[t]) {
                for (const sub of svgSegmentsToSubpaths(svgShapeSegs(kid), m3)) subpaths.push(sub);
              } else collect(kid, ctm2, depth + 1);
            }

          collect(node, base, 0);

        }
      
        function svgOwnBBox(el) {
          const tag = String(el.nodeName || '').toLowerCase();
          const num = (k, d) => {
            const v = parseFloat(el.getAttribute(k));
            return isFinite(v) ? v : d;
          };
          if (tag === 'rect') return { x: num('x', 0), y: num('y', 0), w: num('width', 0), h: num('height', 0) };
          if (tag === 'circle') { const r = num('r', 0); return { x: num('cx', 0) - r, y: num('cy', 0) - r, w: 2 * r, h: 2 * r }; }
          if (tag === 'ellipse') {
            const rx = num('rx', 0), ry = num('ry', 0);
            return { x: num('cx', 0) - rx, y: num('cy', 0) - ry, w: 2 * rx, h: 2 * ry };
          }
          return null;
        }
      
        // Flatten a subpath to a polygon (for clipping / measurement).
        function svgSubpathPolygon(sub, steps) {
          const n = Math.max(6, steps || 10);
          const out = [];
          const nodes = sub.nodes;
          for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
            const a = nodes[i];
            const b = nodes[(i + 1) % nodes.length];
            if (!(sub.closed) && i === nodes.length - 1) break;
            out.push({ x: a.x, y: a.y });
            if (a.o || b.i) {
              const c1 = a.o || a;
              const c2 = b.i || b;
              for (let s = 1; s < n; s++) {
                const t = s / n, u = 1 - t;
                out.push({
                  x: u * u * u * a.x + 3 * u * u * t * c1.x + 3 * u * t * t * c2.x + t * t * t * b.x,
                  y: u * u * u * a.y + 3 * u * u * t * c1.y + 3 * u * t * t * c2.y + t * t * t * b.y
                });
              }
            }
          }
          return out;

        // Real clip: intersection of the shape and its clip outline. Runs on the
        // exact engine (PathBool) — the input subpaths are flattened polygons, but
        // the operation itself keeps full float precision instead of Clipper's
        // 0.001 mm integer grid.
        function svgClipSubpaths(subpaths, clipSubpaths) {
          const PB = pathBoolLib();
          if (!PB) return null;
          const toPath = (subs) => subs
            .filter(s => s.closed && s.nodes.length >= 3)
            .map(s => {
              // curves flatten first (same as the Clipper version): the clip itself
              // runs on polygons, at full float precision
              const pts = svgSubpathPolygon(s, 12);
              if (pts.length < 3) return null;
              return [...PB.pathFromCommands([
                ['M', [pts[0].x, pts[0].y]],
                ...pts.slice(1).map(p => ['L', [p.x, p.y]]),
                ['Z']
              ])];
            })
            .filter(Boolean);
          const subject = toPath(subpaths);
          const clipPaths = toPath(clipSubpaths);
          if (!subject.length || !clipPaths.length) return [];
          // all subject rings travel as ONE path (union by NonZero), all clip rings
          // as the other — the same subject ∩ clip semantics as the Clipper version
          const result = (() => {
            try {
              return new PB.PathBoolean([
                { path: subject.flat(), fillRule: PB.FillRule.NonZero },
                { path: clipPaths.flat(), fillRule: PB.FillRule.NonZero }
              ]).get(PB.PathBooleanOperation.Intersection);
            } catch (err) {
              console.warn('PathBool clip failed:', err);
              return null;
            }
          })();
          if (!result) return null;
          // split the result back into subpaths and hand them out as polyline nodes
          const out = [];
          for (const path of result) {
            let cur = null;
            for (const seg of path) {
              const st = seg[1];
              const en = seg[0] === 'L' ? seg[2]
                : seg[0] === 'C' ? seg[4]
                : seg[0] === 'Q' ? seg[3] : seg[7];
              if (!cur || Math.hypot(cur[cur.length - 1].x - st[0], cur[cur.length - 1].y - st[1]) > 1e-6) {
                if (cur && cur.length >= 3) out.push({ nodes: cur, closed: true, curved: false });
                cur = [{ x: st[0], y: st[1], i: null, o: null }];
              }
              cur.push({ x: en[0], y: en[1], i: null, o: null });
            }
            if (cur && cur.length >= 3) out.push({ nodes: cur, closed: true, curved: false });
          }
          return out;
        }
      
        // --- document level ----------------------------------------------------------
        const SVG_DEFAULT_STYLE = {
          stroke: 'none', fill: '#000000', fillSet: false, strokeWidth: '1', fillRule: 'nonzero',
          display: null, visibility: null, opacity: null
        };
      
        function svgCollectBounds(items) {
          let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
          const put = (x, y) => {
            if (!isFinite(x) || !isFinite(y)) return;
            if (x < minX) minX = x;
            if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
            if (y < minY) minY = y;
            if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
          };
          for (const item of items) {
            if (item.kind === 'circle') {
              put(item.c.x - item.r, item.c.y - item.r);
              put(item.c.x + item.r, item.c.y + item.r);
            } else {
              for (const sub of (item.subpaths || [])) {
                for (const n of sub.nodes) {
                  put(n.x, n.y);
                  if (n.i) put(n.i.x, n.i.y);
                  if (n.o) put(n.o.x, n.o.y);
                }
              }
            }
          }
          if (!isFinite(minX)) return null;
          return { minX, minY, maxX, maxY, w: maxX - minX, h: maxY - minY };
        }
      
        // Parse an SVG string into world-space items (no document changes yet).
        function svgImportParse(text, opts) {
          const o = opts || {};
          const parser = new DOMParser();
          const doc = parser.parseFromString(String(text || ''), 'image/svg+xml');
          const err = doc.getElementsByTagName('parsererror');
          if (err && err.length) throw new Error('SVG could not be parsed');
          const root = doc.documentElement;
          if (!root || String(root.nodeName).toLowerCase() !== 'svg') throw new Error('Not an SVG document');
      
          const byId = new Map();
          const all = root.getElementsByTagName('*');
          for (let i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
            const id = svgLocalId(all[i]);
            if (id && !byId.has(id)) byId.set(id, all[i]);
          }
          const styleNodes = root.getElementsByTagName('style');
          let rules = [];
          for (let i = 0; i < styleNodes.length; i++) {
            rules = rules.concat(svgParseStyleSheets(styleNodes[i].textContent || ''));
          }
      
          // user units -> mm, and flip Y so the sketch stays Y-up
          const vbRaw = String(root.getAttribute('viewBox') || '').trim();
          const vb = vbRaw ? vbRaw.split(/[\s,]+/).map(Number) : null;
          const wMm = svgLenToMm(root.getAttribute('width'));
          const hMm = svgLenToMm(root.getAttribute('height'));
          let scale = 1;
          if (vb && vb.length === 4 && vb[2] > 0 && vb[3] > 0) {
            if (wMm > 0) scale = wMm / vb[2];
            else if (hMm > 0) scale = hMm / vb[3];
          } else if (wMm > 0 && !vb) {
            scale = 1;                                // already mm through the width unit
          }
          if (isFinite(o.scale) && o.scale > 0) scale *= o.scale;
          const vbX = vb && isFinite(vb[0]) ? vb[0] : 0;
          const vbY = vb && isFinite(vb[1]) ? vb[1] : 0;
          // Default flip: file SVG is Y-down (screen convention), the sketch is Y-up.
          // o.flipY === false reads the SVG AS Y-up already (AI-authored drawings):
          // y' = scale*(y - vbY), so the model writes coordinates the way it thinks
          // — bottom-left origin, up is +Y — and nothing arrives mirrored.
          const base = o.flipY === false
            ? [scale, 0, 0, scale, -vbX * scale, -vbY * scale]
            : [scale, 0, 0, -scale, -vbX * scale, vbY * scale];
      
          const ctx = {
            rules,
            byId,
            items: [],
            colors: new Map(),
            skipped: { text: 0, image: 0, use: 0, clip: 0 },
            groupSeq: 0,
            groupNames: {},
            useDepth: 0,
            prefix: o.prefix || 'imp'
          };
      
          if (String(root.getAttribute('display') || '') === 'none' ||
            String(root.getAttribute('visibility') || '') === 'hidden') {
            throw new Error('SVG is hidden');
          }
      
          svgWalk(root, ctx, base, SVG_DEFAULT_STYLE, null, 0);
      
          let segments = 0;
          for (const item of ctx.items) {
            if (item.kind === 'circle') { segments += 4; continue; }
            for (const sub of (item.subpaths || [])) segments += Math.max(1, sub.nodes.length);
          }
          const bounds = svgCollectBounds(ctx.items);
          const colors = Array.from(ctx.colors.entries()).map(([hex, count]) => ({ hex, count }))
            .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count);
          return {
            items: ctx.items,
            bounds: bounds,
            scale: scale,
            mmW: bounds ? bounds.w : 0,
            mmH: bounds ? bounds.h : 0,
            colorList: colors,
            segments: segments,
            skipped: ctx.skipped,
            groupNames: ctx.groupNames,
            groupCount: Object.keys(ctx.groupNames).length
          };
        }
      
        // Insert the parsed items into the sketch. One undo step, called by the dialog.
        function svgImportInsert(ir, opts) {
          const o = opts || {};
          const fillMode = o.fillMode || 'gray';
          const colorMap = o.colorMap || {};
          const keepGroups = !!o.keepGroups;
          const target = o.center || s2w(W / 2, H / 2);
          const bb = ir.bounds || { minX: 0, minY: 0, maxX: 0, maxY: 0 };
          const off = { x: target.x - (bb.minX + bb.maxX) / 2, y: target.y - (bb.minY + bb.maxY) / 2 };
      
          const cache = new Map();
          const mkPoint = (x, y) => {
            const key = Math.round(x * 1e6) + ',' + Math.round(y * 1e6);
            if (cache.has(key)) return cache.get(key);
            const id = privatePoint(x, y);
            cache.set(key, id);
            return id;
          };
          const colorOfSrc = (hex) => colorMap[hex] || hex;
          const made = { entities: [], groups: new Set(), lines: 0, splines: 0, circles: 0, framed: 0 };
          const groupBag = new Map();
      
          const tagEntity = (ent, item) => {
            if (item.group) {
              ent.group = item.group;
              made.groups.add(item.group);
            }
            return ent;
          };
      
          const addSubpaths = (subs, item, forceFlat) => {
            let firstId = null;
            for (const sub of subs) {
              if (!sub.nodes || sub.nodes.length < 2) continue;
              // A path that stops within the join tolerance of where it began is closed,
              // whichever way the exporter wrote it (Z, or just a hairline gap).
              const subFirst = sub.nodes[0];
              const subLast = sub.nodes[sub.nodes.length - 1];
              const nearClosed = !sub.closed && sub.nodes.length > 2 &&
                Math.hypot(subFirst.x - subLast.x, subFirst.y - subLast.y) < JOIN_TOL;
              const isClosed = !!sub.closed || nearClosed;
              if (sub.curved && !forceFlat) {
                const pids = sub.nodes.map(n => mkPoint(n.x + off.x, n.y + off.y));
                if (new Set(pids).size < 2) continue;
                const hs = { o: [], i: [] };
                for (const n of sub.nodes) {
                  hs.o.push(n.o ? { x: n.o.x + off.x, y: n.o.y + off.y } : null);
                  hs.i.push(n.i ? { x: n.i.x + off.x, y: n.i.y + off.y } : null);
                }
                const id = newId();
                state.entities[id] = tagEntity({
                  id, type: 'spline', pts: pids, hs, closed: isClosed,
                  color: item.color, layer: ui.activeLayer
                }, item);
                if (!firstId) firstId = id;
                made.entities.push(id);
                made.splines++;
                continue;
              }
              const pids = sub.nodes.map(n => mkPoint(n.x + off.x, n.y + off.y));
              const chain = isClosed ? pids.concat([pids[0]]) : pids;
              for (let i = 1; i < chain.length; i++) {
                if (chain[i - 1] === chain[i]) continue;
                const id = newId();
                state.entities[id] = tagEntity({
                  id, type: 'line', a: chain[i - 1], b: chain[i],
                  color: item.color, layer: ui.activeLayer
                }, item);
                if (!firstId) firstId = id;
                made.entities.push(id);
                made.lines++;
              }
            }
            if (firstId && item.filled && fillMode !== 'ignore') state.entities[firstId].fill = fillMode;
            return firstId;
          };
      
          for (const item of ir.items) {
            const color = colorOfSrc(item.color);
            const meta = { color, filled: item.filled, group: keepGroups ? null : item.group };
      
            if (item.kind === 'circle') {
              const cid = mkPoint(item.c.x + off.x, item.c.y + off.y);
              const id = newId();
              state.entities[id] = tagEntity({
                id, type: 'circle', c: cid, r: item.r, color, layer: ui.activeLayer
              }, meta);
              if (item.filled && fillMode !== 'ignore') state.entities[id].fill = fillMode;
              made.entities.push(id);
              made.circles++;
              continue;
            }
      
            let subs = item.subpaths || [];
            if (item.clip && subs.length) {
              const clipped = svgClipSubpaths(subs, item.clip);
              if (clipped) subs = clipped; else ir.skipped.clip++;
            }
            if (!subs.length) continue;
      
            if (keepGroups && item.group) {
              const bag = groupBag.get(item.group) || { color, filled: false, contours: [], curves: [] };
              bag.filled = bag.filled || item.filled;
              for (const sub of subs) {
                const pts = svgSubpathPolygon(sub, 12).map(p => ({ x: p.x + off.x, y: p.y + off.y }));
                if (pts.length >= 2) bag.contours.push({ pts, closed: !!sub.closed });

                // so Ctrl+Shift+G can rebuild the original shapes instead of a flat
                // approximation of them
                bag.curves.push(sub.curved ? {
                  closed: !!sub.closed,
                  curved: true,
                  nodes: sub.nodes.map(n => ({
                    x: n.x + off.x, y: n.y + off.y,
                    o: n.o ? { x: n.o.x + off.x, y: n.o.y + off.y } : null,
                    i: n.i ? { x: n.i.x + off.x, y: n.i.y + off.y } : null
                  }))
                } : {
                  closed: !!sub.closed,
                  curved: false,
                  nodes: sub.nodes.map(n => ({ x: n.x + off.x, y: n.y + off.y, o: null, i: null }))
                });
              }
              groupBag.set(item.group, bag);
              continue;
            }
      
            addSubpaths(subs, meta, false);
          }
      
          for (const [group, bag] of groupBag) {
            if (!bag.contours.length) continue;
            const id = addFramedObject(bag.contours, {
              color: bag.color,
              layer: ui.activeLayer,
              name: ir.groupNames[group] || '',
              tol: ART_TOL_MM
            });
            if (id != null) {
              const ent = state.entities[id];
              if (ent) {
                ent.group = group;
                if (bag.filled && fillMode !== 'ignore') ent.fill = fillMode;
              }
              made.entities.push(id);

        // --- dialog -------------------------------------------------------------------
        let svgImportJob = null;
      
        function openSvgImport(file) {
          file.text().then((text) => {
            let ir = null;

              ir = svgImportParse(text, { prefix: 'i' + Date.now().toString(36) + '-' });
            } catch (err) {
              toast('That SVG could not be read (' + (err && err.message ? err.message : 'parse error') + ')');
              return;
            }
            if (!ir.items.length) {
              const bits = [];
              if (ir.skipped.text) bits.push(ir.skipped.text + ' text');
              if (ir.skipped.image) bits.push(ir.skipped.image + ' image');
              toast('No vector shapes found' + (bits.length ? ' (skipped ' + bits.join(', ') + ')' : ''));
              return;
            }
            svgImportJob = { name: file.name, ir: ir };
            buildSvgImportDialog();
            const modal = document.getElementById('svg-import-modal');
            if (modal) modal.classList.remove('hidden');
          }).catch(() => toast('Could not read that file'));
        }
      
        function closeSvgImport() {
          const modal = document.getElementById('svg-import-modal');
          if (modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
          svgImportJob = null;
        }
      
        function buildSvgImportDialog() {
          const job = svgImportJob;
          if (!job) return;
          const ir = job.ir;
          const nameEl = document.getElementById('imp-file');
          if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = job.name;
      
          const bits = [];
          bits.push(fmt(ir.mmW) + ' × ' + fmt(ir.mmH) + ' mm');
          bits.push(ir.items.length + (ir.items.length === 1 ? ' shape' : ' shapes'));
          if (ir.segments) bits.push(ir.segments + ' segments');
          if (ir.groupCount) bits.push(ir.groupCount + (ir.groupCount === 1 ? ' group' : ' groups'));
          if (ir.skipped.text) bits.push(ir.skipped.text + ' text skipped');
          if (ir.skipped.image) bits.push(ir.skipped.image + ' image skipped');
          if (ir.skipped.use) bits.push(ir.skipped.use + ' unresolved use');
          const sumEl = document.getElementById('imp-summary');
          if (sumEl) sumEl.textContent = bits.join(' · ');
          const warnEl = document.getElementById('imp-warn');
          if (warnEl) {
            const heavy = ir.segments > 8000;
            warnEl.hidden = !heavy;
            warnEl.textContent = heavy
              ? 'Large file: ' + ir.segments + ' segments. The window will freeze for a few seconds while it is built — turn "keep each group as one object" on to make it much lighter.'
              : '';
          }
      
          const host = document.getElementById('imp-colors');
          if (host) {
            host.innerHTML = '';
            for (const entry of ir.colorList) {
              const row = document.createElement('label');
              row.className = 'imp-color';
              const sw = document.createElement('i');
              sw.className = 'imp-swatch';
              sw.style.background = entry.hex;
              const code = document.createElement('code');
              code.textContent = entry.hex;
              const count = document.createElement('span');
              count.className = 'imp-count';
              count.textContent = entry.count + '×';
              const sel = document.createElement('select');
              sel.dataset.src = entry.hex;
              const picked = nearestCncColor(entry.hex);
              for (const c of COLORS) {
                const opt = document.createElement('option');
                opt.value = c.value;
                opt.textContent = c.name;
                if (c.value === picked) opt.selected = true;
                sel.appendChild(opt);
              }
              row.appendChild(sw);
              row.appendChild(code);
              row.appendChild(count);
              row.appendChild(sel);
              host.appendChild(row);
            }
            if (!ir.colorList.length) {
              const none = document.createElement('div');
              none.className = 'imp-count';
              none.textContent = 'No stroke or fill colours — everything comes in as cut (black).';
              host.appendChild(none);
            }
          }
        }
      
        function runSvgImport() {
          const job = svgImportJob;
          if (!job) return;
          const keepGroups = !!(document.getElementById('imp-keep-groups') || {}).checked;
          const fillEl = document.getElementById('imp-fill');
          const fillMode = fillEl ? fillEl.value : 'gray';
          const colorMap = {};
          for (const sel of document.querySelectorAll('#imp-colors select[data-src]')) {
            colorMap[sel.dataset.src] = sel.value;
          }
          const wasEmpty = !Object.keys(state.entities).length;
          pushHistory();
          let made = null;
          try {
            made = svgImportInsert(job.ir, { keepGroups, fillMode, colorMap });
          } catch (err) {
            history.pop();
            updateHistoryButtons();
            toast('Import failed: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : 'unknown error'));
            return;
          }
          ui.selection = made.entities.map(id => ({ kind: 'entity', id }));
          invalidateLoops();
          commit();
          if (wasEmpty) fitView();
          const parts = [];
          if (made.lines) parts.push(made.lines + ' line' + (made.lines === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          if (made.splines) parts.push(made.splines + ' curve' + (made.splines === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          if (made.circles) parts.push(made.circles + ' circle' + (made.circles === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          if (made.framed) parts.push(made.framed + ' framed object' + (made.framed === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          const skipped = job.ir.skipped;
          const sk = [];
          if (skipped.text) sk.push(skipped.text + ' text');
          if (skipped.image) sk.push(skipped.image + ' image');
          if (skipped.clip) sk.push(skipped.clip + ' clip could not be cut');
          toast('Imported ' + (parts.length ? parts.join(' · ') : 'nothing') +
            (made.groups.size ? ' · ' + made.groups.size + ' group' + (made.groups.size === 1 ? '' : 's') : '') +
            (sk.length ? ' · skipped ' + sk.join(', ') : ''));
          closeSvgImport();
        }
      
      
        // ---- Settings -------------------------------------------------------------
        // One place for the numeric knobs. A new knob only needs an entry here: the
        // window builds itself from SETTINGS_SPEC and applySettings() pushes the values
        // into the working constants.
        const SETTINGS_KEY = 'minisketch.settings.v1';
      
        const SETTINGS_SPEC = [
          { group: 'History', key: 'undoRestoresView', label: 'Undo moves the view', unit: '',
            def: 'off', choices: [['off', 'Off \u2014 keep the camera'], ['on', 'On \u2014 restore it']], hidden: true,
            help: 'When On, Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Shift+Z also restore the viewport that was in effect when the step was made. (Toggled from the Tool menu.)' },
          { group: 'Renderer', key: 'pointSize', label: 'Point size', unit: 'px',
            def: 6.4, min: 2, max: 20, step: 0.2,

            def: 50, min: 10, max: 200, step: 5,
            help: 'Marker size follows the zoom (size x zoom) but never shrinks below this percentage of the base size.' },
          { group: 'Renderer', key: 'pointZoomMax', label: 'Point max at zoom', unit: '%',
            def: 125, min: 50, max: 500, step: 5,

          { group: 'Renderer', key: 'lineWidth', label: 'Line width', unit: 'px',
            def: 2, min: 0.5, max: 10, step: 0.25,
            help: 'Stroke width of the geometry at 100% zoom.' },
          { group: 'Renderer', key: 'partWidth', label: 'Part stroke', unit: 'px',

          { group: 'Renderer', key: 'renderScale', label: 'Canvas resolution', unit: '%',
            def: '100', choices: [['100', '100'], ['75', '75'], ['50', '50']],
