          svgWalk(root, ctx, base, SVG_DEFAULT_STYLE, null, 0);
      
          let segments = 0;
          for (const item of ctx.items) {
            if (item.kind === 'circle') { segments += 4; continue; }
            for (const sub of (item.subpaths || [])) segments += Math.max(1, sub.nodes.length);
          }
          const bounds = svgCollectBounds(ctx.items);
          const colors = Array.from(ctx.colors.entries()).map(([hex, count]) => ({ hex, count }))
            .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count);
          return {
            items: ctx.items,
            bounds: bounds,
            scale: scale,
            mmW: bounds ? bounds.w : 0,
            mmH: bounds ? bounds.h : 0,
            colorList: colors,
            segments: segments,
            skipped: ctx.skipped,
            groupNames: ctx.groupNames,
            groupCount: Object.keys(ctx.groupNames).length
          };
        }
      
        // Insert the parsed items into the sketch. One undo step, called by the dialog.
        function svgImportInsert(ir, opts) {
          const o = opts || {};
          const fillMode = o.fillMode || 'gray';
          const colorMap = o.colorMap || {};
          const keepGroups = !!o.keepGroups;
          const target = o.center || s2w(W / 2, H / 2);
          const bb = ir.bounds || { minX: 0, minY: 0, maxX: 0, maxY: 0 };
          const off = { x: target.x - (bb.minX + bb.maxX) / 2, y: target.y - (bb.minY + bb.maxY) / 2 };
      
          const cache = new Map();
          const mkPoint = (x, y) => {
            const key = Math.round(x * 1e6) + ',' + Math.round(y * 1e6);
            if (cache.has(key)) return cache.get(key);
            const id = privatePoint(x, y);
            cache.set(key, id);
            return id;
          };
          const colorOfSrc = (hex) => colorMap[hex] || hex;
          const made = { entities: [], groups: new Set(), lines: 0, splines: 0, circles: 0, framed: 0 };
          const groupBag = new Map();
      
          const tagEntity = (ent, item) => {
            if (item.group) {
              ent.group = item.group;
              made.groups.add(item.group);
            }
            return ent;
          };
      
          const addSubpaths = (subs, item, forceFlat) => {
            let firstId = null;
            for (const sub of subs) {
              if (!sub.nodes || sub.nodes.length < 2) continue;
              // A path that stops within the join tolerance of where it began is closed,
              // whichever way the exporter wrote it (Z, or just a hairline gap).
              const subFirst = sub.nodes[0];
              const subLast = sub.nodes[sub.nodes.length - 1];
              const nearClosed = !sub.closed && sub.nodes.length > 2 &&
                Math.hypot(subFirst.x - subLast.x, subFirst.y - subLast.y) < JOIN_TOL;
              const isClosed = !!sub.closed || nearClosed;
              if (sub.curved && !forceFlat) {
                const pids = sub.nodes.map(n => mkPoint(n.x + off.x, n.y + off.y));
                if (new Set(pids).size < 2) continue;
                const hs = { o: [], i: [] };
                for (const n of sub.nodes) {
                  hs.o.push(n.o ? { x: n.o.x + off.x, y: n.o.y + off.y } : null);
                  hs.i.push(n.i ? { x: n.i.x + off.x, y: n.i.y + off.y } : null);
                }
                const id = newId();
                state.entities[id] = tagEntity({
                  id, type: 'spline', pts: pids, hs, closed: isClosed,
                  color: item.color, layer: ui.activeLayer
                }, item);
                if (!firstId) firstId = id;
                made.entities.push(id);
                made.splines++;
                continue;
              }
              const pids = sub.nodes.map(n => mkPoint(n.x + off.x, n.y + off.y));
              const chain = isClosed ? pids.concat([pids[0]]) : pids;
              for (let i = 1; i < chain.length; i++) {
                if (chain[i - 1] === chain[i]) continue;
                const id = newId();
                state.entities[id] = tagEntity({
                  id, type: 'line', a: chain[i - 1], b: chain[i],
                  color: item.color, layer: ui.activeLayer
                }, item);
                if (!firstId) firstId = id;
                made.entities.push(id);
                made.lines++;
              }
            }
            if (firstId && item.filled && fillMode !== 'ignore') state.entities[firstId].fill = fillMode;
            return firstId;
          };
      
          for (const item of ir.items) {
            const color = colorOfSrc(item.color);
            const meta = { color, filled: item.filled, group: keepGroups ? null : item.group };
      
            if (item.kind === 'circle') {
              const cid = mkPoint(item.c.x + off.x, item.c.y + off.y);
              const id = newId();
              state.entities[id] = tagEntity({
                id, type: 'circle', c: cid, r: item.r, color, layer: ui.activeLayer
              }, meta);
              if (item.filled && fillMode !== 'ignore') state.entities[id].fill = fillMode;
              made.entities.push(id);
              made.circles++;
              continue;
            }
      
            let subs = item.subpaths || [];
            if (item.clip && subs.length) {
              const clipped = svgClipSubpaths(subs, item.clip);
              if (clipped) subs = clipped; else ir.skipped.clip++;
            }
            if (!subs.length) continue;
      
            if (keepGroups && item.group) {
              const bag = groupBag.get(item.group) || { color, filled: false, contours: [], curves: [] };
              bag.filled = bag.filled || item.filled;
              for (const sub of subs) {
                const pts = svgSubpathPolygon(sub, 12).map(p => ({ x: p.x + off.x, y: p.y + off.y }));
                if (pts.length >= 2) bag.contours.push({ pts, closed: !!sub.closed });
                // keep the real curve data (or the exact polyline) of every subpath,
                // so Ctrl+Shift+G can rebuild the original shapes instead of a flat
                // approximation of them
                bag.curves.push(sub.curved ? {
                  closed: !!sub.closed,
                  curved: true,
                  nodes: sub.nodes.map(n => ({
                    x: n.x + off.x, y: n.y + off.y,
                    o: n.o ? { x: n.o.x + off.x, y: n.o.y + off.y } : null,
                    i: n.i ? { x: n.i.x + off.x, y: n.i.y + off.y } : null
                  }))
                } : {
                  closed: !!sub.closed,
                  curved: false,
                  nodes: sub.nodes.map(n => ({ x: n.x + off.x, y: n.y + off.y, o: null, i: null }))
                });
              }
              groupBag.set(item.group, bag);
              continue;
            }
      
            addSubpaths(subs, meta, false);
          }
      
          for (const [group, bag] of groupBag) {
            if (!bag.contours.length) continue;
            const id = addFramedObject(bag.contours, {
              color: bag.color,
              layer: ui.activeLayer,
              name: ir.groupNames[group] || '',
              tol: ART_TOL_MM
            });
            if (id != null) {
              const ent = state.entities[id];
              if (ent) {
                ent.group = group;
                if (bag.filled && fillMode !== 'ignore') ent.fill = fillMode;
              }
              made.entities.push(id);
              made.groups.add(group);
              made.framed++;
            }
          }
      
          return made;
        }
      
        // --- dialog -------------------------------------------------------------------
        let svgImportJob = null;
      
        function openSvgImport(file) {
          file.text().then((text) => {
            let ir = null;
            try {
              ir = svgImportParse(text, { prefix: 'i' + Date.now().toString(36) + '-' });
            } catch (err) {
              toast('That SVG could not be read (' + (err && err.message ? err.message : 'parse error') + ')');
              return;
            }
            if (!ir.items.length) {
              const bits = [];
              if (ir.skipped.text) bits.push(ir.skipped.text + ' text');
              if (ir.skipped.image) bits.push(ir.skipped.image + ' image');
              toast('No vector shapes found' + (bits.length ? ' (skipped ' + bits.join(', ') + ')' : ''));
              return;
            }
            svgImportJob = { name: file.name, ir: ir };
            buildSvgImportDialog();
            const modal = document.getElementById('svg-import-modal');
            if (modal) modal.classList.remove('hidden');
          }).catch(() => toast('Could not read that file'));
        }
      
        function closeSvgImport() {
          const modal = document.getElementById('svg-import-modal');
          if (modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
          svgImportJob = null;
        }
      
        function buildSvgImportDialog() {
          const job = svgImportJob;
          if (!job) return;
          const ir = job.ir;
          const nameEl = document.getElementById('imp-file');
          if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = job.name;
      
          const bits = [];
          bits.push(fmt(ir.mmW) + ' × ' + fmt(ir.mmH) + ' mm');
          bits.push(ir.items.length + (ir.items.length === 1 ? ' shape' : ' shapes'));
          if (ir.segments) bits.push(ir.segments + ' segments');
          if (ir.groupCount) bits.push(ir.groupCount + (ir.groupCount === 1 ? ' group' : ' groups'));
          if (ir.skipped.text) bits.push(ir.skipped.text + ' text skipped');
          if (ir.skipped.image) bits.push(ir.skipped.image + ' image skipped');
          if (ir.skipped.use) bits.push(ir.skipped.use + ' unresolved use');
          const sumEl = document.getElementById('imp-summary');
          if (sumEl) sumEl.textContent = bits.join(' · ');
          const warnEl = document.getElementById('imp-warn');
          if (warnEl) {
            const heavy = ir.segments > 8000;
            warnEl.hidden = !heavy;
            warnEl.textContent = heavy
              ? 'Large file: ' + ir.segments + ' segments. The window will freeze for a few seconds while it is built — turn "keep each group as one object" on to make it much lighter.'
              : '';
          }
      
          const host = document.getElementById('imp-colors');
          if (host) {
            host.innerHTML = '';
            for (const entry of ir.colorList) {
              const row = document.createElement('label');
              row.className = 'imp-color';
              const sw = document.createElement('i');
              sw.className = 'imp-swatch';
              sw.style.background = entry.hex;
              const code = document.createElement('code');
              code.textContent = entry.hex;
              const count = document.createElement('span');
              count.className = 'imp-count';
              count.textContent = entry.count + '×';
              const sel = document.createElement('select');
              sel.dataset.src = entry.hex;
              const picked = nearestCncColor(entry.hex);
              for (const c of COLORS) {
                const opt = document.createElement('option');
                opt.value = c.value;
                opt.textContent = c.name;
                if (c.value === picked) opt.selected = true;
                sel.appendChild(opt);
              }
              row.appendChild(sw);
              row.appendChild(code);
              row.appendChild(count);
              row.appendChild(sel);
              host.appendChild(row);
            }
            if (!ir.colorList.length) {
              const none = document.createElement('div');
              none.className = 'imp-count';
              none.textContent = 'No stroke or fill colours — everything comes in as cut (black).';
              host.appendChild(none);
            }
          }
        }
      
        function runSvgImport() {
          const job = svgImportJob;
          if (!job) return;
          const keepGroups = !!(document.getElementById('imp-keep-groups') || {}).checked;
          const fillEl = document.getElementById('imp-fill');
          const fillMode = fillEl ? fillEl.value : 'gray';
          const colorMap = {};
          for (const sel of document.querySelectorAll('#imp-colors select[data-src]')) {
            colorMap[sel.dataset.src] = sel.value;
          }
          const wasEmpty = !Object.keys(state.entities).length;
          pushHistory();
          let made = null;
          try {
            made = svgImportInsert(job.ir, { keepGroups, fillMode, colorMap });
          } catch (err) {
            history.pop();
            updateHistoryButtons();
            toast('Import failed: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : 'unknown error'));
            return;
          }
          ui.selection = made.entities.map(id => ({ kind: 'entity', id }));
          invalidateLoops();
          commit();
          if (wasEmpty) fitView();
          const parts = [];
          if (made.lines) parts.push(made.lines + ' line' + (made.lines === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          if (made.splines) parts.push(made.splines + ' curve' + (made.splines === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          if (made.circles) parts.push(made.circles + ' circle' + (made.circles === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          if (made.framed) parts.push(made.framed + ' framed object' + (made.framed === 1 ? '' : 's'));
          const skipped = job.ir.skipped;
          const sk = [];
          if (skipped.text) sk.push(skipped.text + ' text');
          if (skipped.image) sk.push(skipped.image + ' image');
          if (skipped.clip) sk.push(skipped.clip + ' clip could not be cut');
          toast('Imported ' + (parts.length ? parts.join(' · ') : 'nothing') +
            (made.groups.size ? ' · ' + made.groups.size + ' group' + (made.groups.size === 1 ? '' : 's') : '') +
            (sk.length ? ' · skipped ' + sk.join(', ') : ''));
          closeSvgImport();
        }

  function onPointerDown(ev) {
    if (ui.offsetJob) return;                 // drafting: wheel and Enter only
    if (ev.button === 1) {
      // middle click = the loop below (Alt+click does the same, but many Linux
      // desktops steal Alt+drag for moving the window)
      ev.preventDefault();
      selectBelow(ev);
      return;
    }
    if (ev.button === 2) {
      if (ui.tool === 'transform') {
        ev.preventDefault();
        if (ui.transform.pending) xfCancel();
        else exitTransformMode();
        return;
      }
      if (ui.draft) {
        if (ui.draft.type === 'curve') finishCurve(false);
        else cancelDraft();
        render();
        return;
      }
      if (ui.tool !== 'select') {
        setTool('select');
        return;
      }
      ev.preventDefault();
      startPan(pointerPos(ev));
      canvas.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId);
      return;
    }
    if (ev.button !== 0) return;
    const pos = pointerPos(ev);
    const world = s2w(pos.x, pos.y);
    if (ui.nestPlace) {
      ev.preventDefault();
      const ir = ui.nestPlace;
      ui.nestPlace = null;
      nestPlaceAt(ir, world);
      render();
      return;
    }
    if (ui.moveJob) {
      ev.preventDefault();
      const snap = snapPoint(world, { skip: new Set() });
      const pt = snap && isFinite(+snap.x) && isFinite(+snap.y) ? snap : world;
      if (ui.moveJob.stage === 'ref') {
        ui.moveJob.ref = { x: pt.x, y: pt.y };
        ui.moveJob.stage = 'dst';
        updateUI();
        toast('Move: click the destination point · Esc cancels');
      } else {
        const ref = ui.moveJob.ref || world;
        const dx0 = pt.x - ref.x;
        const dy0 = pt.y - ref.y;
        ui.moveJob = null;
        const typed = prompt('Move dx, dy (mm)', fmt(dx0) + ', ' + fmt(dy0));
        updateUI();
        if (typed == null) return;
        const nums = String(typed).split(/[\s,;]+/).map(Number).filter(v => isFinite(v));
        if (!nums.length) { toast('No movement entered'); return; }
        applyMoveDelta(nums[0], nums.length > 1 ? nums[1] : 0);
      }
      render();
      return;
    }
    // Ctrl = add to the selection, Shift = subtract from it
    const addMod = ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey;
    const subMod = ev.shiftKey;
    updateCoords(pos.x, pos.y);
    canvas.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId);
    // The modifier flags are also the drafting overrides (Shift = polar,
    // Alt = snap off): refresh them from the event so a stale flag from an
    // earlier move can never silently switch snapping off for this click.
    ui.shiftDown = ev.shiftKey;
    ui.altDown = ev.altKey;

    if (ui.tool === 'transform') {
      const grip = xfHit(pos.x, pos.y);
      if (grip) {
        xfBeginDrag(grip, pos, world);
        xfUpdatePanel();
        render();
      } else if (ui.transform.pending) {
        toast('Apply or cancel the transform first');
      }
      return;
    }

    if (ui.tool === 'select') {
      // Mate job: every click in this mode is a part-marker pick. Side B may

      if (mateJob) {
        // Near a marker it is a marker pick; near a bare edge (and NOT near any

              moved: false
            };

            ui.drag = {

  function handleCreateClick(snap, ev) {
    const shift = ev.shiftKey;

    if (ui.tool === 'text') {
      pushHistory();
      const thH = loadTextHeight();
      const p0 = materializePoint(snap);
      const pwId = privatePoint(snap.x + 60, snap.y);
      const phId = privatePoint(snap.x, snap.y - thH);
      // Two real guide lines: they carry the H/V/angle/tangent constraints and
      // are green, which KaryaCNC reads as "do not machine this path".
      const idW = newId();
      state.entities[idW] = { id: idW, type: 'line', a: p0, b: pwId, color: '#00ff00' };
      const idH = newId();
      state.entities[idH] = { id: idH, type: 'line', a: p0, b: phId, color: '#00ff00' };
      // The two guides start horizontal + perpendicular, so a fresh text sits
      // level and square; both are ordinary constraints (H / [ remove them).
      const cH = newId();
      state.constraints[cH] = { id: cH, type: 'horizontal', entity: idW };
      const cPerp = newId();
      state.constraints[cPerp] = { id: cPerp, type: 'perpendicular', line1: idW, line2: idH };
      // The vertical guide is born dimensioned to the remembered text height
      const cHdim = newId();
      state.constraints[cHdim] = {
        id: cHdim, type: 'distance', p1: p0, p2: phId, value: thH, offset: 26
      };
      const id = newId();
      state.entities[id] = {
        id, type: 'text', p0, pw: pwId, ph: phId, guide: [idW, idH],
        text: 'Text', font: 'sans-serif', weight: 400, italic: false, tracking: 0,
        color: ui.color, fitCurve: loadTextFit()
      };
      ensureTextAspect(state.entities[id]);
      ui.selection = [{ kind: 'entity', id }];
      setTool('select');
      requestSolve();
      commit();
      openTextEditor(id, true);
      return;
    }

    if (ui.tool === 'line') {
      if (!ui.draft || ui.draft.type !== 'line') {
        ui.draft = { type: 'line', a: snap, chain: [] };
      } else {
        const start = ui.draft.a.id != null ? P(ui.draft.a.id) : ui.draft.a;
        if (!start) {
          cancelDraft();
          render();
          return;
        }
        let end = { x: snap.x, y: snap.y, id: snap.id, midId: snap.midId, lineId: snap.lineId, weld: snap.weld };
        if (shift) end = angleSnap(start, end);
        if (Math.hypot(end.x - start.x, end.y - start.y) < 1e-7) return;
        pushHistory();
        const aId = materializePoint(ui.draft.a);
        const bId = materializePoint(end);
        if (aId === bId) {
          history.pop();
          updateHistoryButtons();
          return;
        }
        const id = newId();
        state.entities[id] = { id, type: 'line', a: aId, b: bId, color: ui.color };
        // the vertex this segment started from, so Esc can take the segment
        // back and continue from there (same undo-last-segment as the curve)
        if (!ui.draft.chain) ui.draft.chain = [];
        ui.draft.chain.push({ a: { x: start.x, y: start.y, id: aId }, seg: id });
        ui.draft.a = { x: end.x, y: end.y, id: bId };
        // remember the point the chain started from: landing back on it closes
        // the loop and the draft is done (a third segment at least, so a
        // two-point "closure" — which would only double the first line — still
        // just continues the chain)
        if (ui.draft.chain.length === 1) ui.draft.firstId = aId;
        const closing = ui.draft.firstId != null && bId === ui.draft.firstId &&
          ui.draft.chain.length >= 2;
        requestSolve();
        commit();
        if (closing) {
          cancelDraft();
          toast('Loop closed — the chain is done');
        }
      }
      render();
      return;
    }

    if (ui.tool === 'arc') {
      if (!ui.draft || ui.draft.type !== 'arc') {
        ui.draft = { type: 'arc', stage: 1, c: snap };
      } else if (ui.draft.stage === 1) {
        const c = ui.draft.c.id != null ? P(ui.draft.c.id) : ui.draft.c;
        if (!c) {
          cancelDraft();
          return;
        }
        const end = shift ? angleSnap(c, snap) : snap;
        const r = Math.hypot(end.x - c.x, end.y - c.y);
        if (r < 1e-7) return;
        const sPoint = { x: c.x + (end.x - c.x) / r * r, y: c.y + (end.y - c.y) / r * r, id: end.id };
        ui.draft = { type: 'arc', stage: 2, c: ui.draft.c, s: sPoint };
      } else if (ui.draft.stage === 2) {
        const c = ui.draft.c.id != null ? P(ui.draft.c.id) : ui.draft.c;
        const sPt = ui.draft.s.id != null ? P(ui.draft.s.id) : ui.draft.s;
        if (!c || !sPt) {
          cancelDraft();
          return;
        }
        const r = Math.hypot(sPt.x - c.x, sPt.y - c.y);
        let angle = Math.atan2(snap.y - c.y, snap.x - c.x);
        if (shift) angle = Math.round(angle / (Math.PI / 12)) * (Math.PI / 12);
        const a0 = Math.atan2(sPt.y - c.y, sPt.x - c.x);
        if (Math.abs(wrapPi(angle - a0)) < 1e-7) return;
        pushHistory();
        const cId = materializePoint(ui.draft.c);
        const sId = materializePoint(ui.draft.s);
        const eId = pointAtWorld(c.x + Math.cos(angle) * r, c.y + Math.sin(angle) * r);
        const id = newId();
        state.entities[id] = { id, type: 'arc', c: cId, s: sId, e: eId, color: ui.color };
        ui.draft = null;
        requestSolve();
        commit();
      }
      render();
      return;
    }

    if (ui.tool === 'circle') {
      if (!ui.draft || ui.draft.type !== 'circle') {
        ui.draft = { type: 'circle', c: snap };
      } else {
        const c = ui.draft.c.id != null ? P(ui.draft.c.id) : ui.draft.c;
        const r = Math.hypot(snap.x - c.x, snap.y - c.y);
        if (r < 1e-7) return;
        pushHistory();
        const cId = materializePoint(ui.draft.c);
        const id = newId();
        state.entities[id] = { id, type: 'circle', c: cId, r, color: ui.color };
        ui.draft = null;
        ui.selection = [{ kind: 'entity', id }];
        requestSolve();
        commit();
        // Same as pressing D with the new circle selected: one value sets
        // the radius, two values (100,200) turn it into an ellipse curve.
        createDimension();
      }
      render();
      return;
    }

    if (ui.tool === 'box') {
      if (!ui.draft || ui.draft.type !== 'box') {
        ui.draft = { type: 'box', a: snap };
        ui.boxFillet = 0;
        ui.boxFilletTouched = false;
      } else {
        const a = ui.draft.a.id != null ? P(ui.draft.a.id) : ui.draft.a;
        const x1 = a.x, y1 = a.y;
        const x2 = snap.x, y2 = snap.y;
        if (Math.abs(x2 - x1) < 1e-7 || Math.abs(y2 - y1) < 1e-7) return;
        // No scroll wheel means sharp corners are wanted: skip the radius
        // prompt entirely and just build the box.
        const maxR = Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.abs(x2 - x1), Math.abs(y2 - y1)) / 2 - 0.001);
        let r = Math.min(ui.boxFillet || 0, maxR);
        if (ui.boxFilletTouched) {
          const input = prompt('Fillet radius (0 = sharp corners)', fmt(ui.boxFillet || 0));
          if (input == null) {
            cancelDraft();
            render();
            return;
          }
          const wantR = evalFormula(input, ui.boxFillet || 0);

  function onPointerMove(ev) {
    // A drag owns the geometry until the mouse is released: in 'release' mode
    // solving is postponed to pointerup, which is what makes the drag cheap.
    if (ui.drag) dragSolveSuppressed = true;
    const pos = pointerPos(ev);
    updateCoords(pos.x, pos.y);
    ui.shiftDown = ev.shiftKey;
    ui.altDown = ev.altKey;

    if (ui.pan) {
      view.ox = ui.pan.ox + (pos.x - ui.pan.startX);
      view.oy = ui.pan.oy + (pos.y - ui.pan.startY);
      ui.lastWorld = s2w(pos.x, pos.y);
      scheduleRender();
      scheduleViewSave();
      return;
    }

    // Move job (Ctrl+M): while picking the reference / destination point, the
    // cursor gets the same green point target a single-point drag shows, so an
    // exact snap is visible even when the point is selected (blue)
    if (ui.moveJob) {
      const world = s2w(pos.x, pos.y);
      ui.moveJob.snap = snapPoint(world, { skip: new Set() });
      // With parts selected the move reference also snaps to their markers
      // (a part owns no points, so the plain point snap never sees them)
      if (!ui.moveJob.snap) {
        const pm = hitPartMarker(pos.x, pos.y, null);
        if (pm) {
          const E = state.entities[pm.part];
          if (E) {
            const w = partWorldPoint(E, pm.u, pm.v);
            ui.moveJob.snap = { x: w.x, y: w.y };
          }
        }
      }
      render();
      return;
    }

    const world = s2w(pos.x, pos.y);
    ui.raw = world;
    ui.lastWorld = world;

    if (ui.tool === 'curve' && ui.draft && ui.draft.drag) {
      const dr = ui.draft.drag;
      const node = ui.draft.nodes[dr.idx];
      if (node && Math.hypot(world.x - dr.startX, world.y - dr.startY) > 2 / view.zoom) {
        node.o = { x: world.x, y: world.y };
        node.i = { x: 2 * dr.startX - world.x, y: 2 * dr.startY - world.y };
        dr.moved = true;
      }
      render();
      return;
    }

    if (ui.drag) {
      const d = ui.drag;
      // Part marker drag — the bounded-correction model:
      //   dx,dy  = raw mouse delta from the press (the movement is ALWAYS
      //            this; the part never jumps to the cursor)
      //   dx2,dy2 = press-to-nearest-marker offset, frozen at press
      // Snap: project the marker to where it WOULD be, find a snap near it,
      // and shift the whole part by the difference. One calculation per frame
      // from the start pose — no incremental drift, a caught snap stays caught.
      if (d.kind === 'partMarker') {
        const part = state.entities[d.part];
        if (!part) { ui.drag = null; render(); return; }
        let dx = world.x - d.startWorld.x;
        let dy = world.y - d.startWorld.y;
        if (ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey) {
          if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) dy = 0;
          else dx = 0;
        }
        let sx = 0;
        let sy = 0;
        d.snapAt = null;
        if (d.startPose) {
          // placement drag: where the grabbed marker would land under the raw
          // move, then the bounded snap correction
          const wx = d.grab.x + dx;
          const wy = d.grab.y + dy;
          const snap = findSnapFor({ x: wx, y: wy }, d.part, d.snapCache);
          if (snap && !(Math.abs(snap.x - d.grab.x) < 0.75 &&
            Math.abs(snap.y - d.grab.y) < 0.75)) {
            // shift the whole part so the marker lands on the snap
            sx = snap.x - wx;
            sy = snap.y - wy;
            d.snapAt = snap;
            d.snapPart = true;
          } else {
            d.snapPart = false;
          }
          part.pose = {
            tx: d.startPose.tx + dx + sx,
            ty: d.startPose.ty + dy + sy,
            rot: d.startPose.rot
          };
          invalidateLoops();
        } else {
          // MECHANISM drag (mated part): the raw mouse position is the target
          // for the grabbed marker — the solver simulates the joints live, no
          // placement snap, no bounded correction
          solveAssemblyNow([{ part: d.part, ref: d.ref, to: { x: world.x, y: world.y } }], { fast: true });

  function onPointerUp(ev) {
    // the gesture is over: the solve below is allowed again
    dragSolveSuppressed = false;
    cancelDragAutoSolve();
    if (ui.tool === 'curve' && ui.draft && ui.draft.drag) {
      ui.draft.drag = null;
      render();
      return;
    }
    if (ui.drag && ui.drag.kind && ui.drag.kind.indexOf('xf') === 0) {
      const d = ui.drag;
      ui.drag = null;
      const p = ui.transform.pending;
      if (p && d.kind !== 'xfPivot' && !p.moved) xfDiscardEmpty();
      updateCanvasCursor();
      xfUpdatePanel();
      render();
      return;
    }
    if (ui.drag && ui.drag.kind === 'rubber') {
      const d = ui.drag;
      ui.drag = null;
      const p = pointerPos(ev);
      const moved = Math.abs(p.x - d.start.x) > 3 || Math.abs(p.y - d.start.y) > 3;
      if (d.deferredLoop && !moved) {
        const m = d.deferredMods || {};
        applyLoopClick(d.deferredLoop, !!m.add, !!m.sub, !!m.alt);
        return;
      }
      applyRubberSelection(d, p);
      return;
    }
    if (ui.pan) {
      ui.pan = null;
      updateCanvasCursor();
      scheduleViewSave();
      render();
      return;
    }
    if (ui.drag) {
      const d = ui.drag;
      // A part-marker drag moved pose data, not points: no constraint solve,
      // no loop invalidation beyond the pose footprint change. The release
      // runs the FULL alternating solve (the interactive passes were fast).
      if (d.kind === 'partMarker' || d.kind === 'partBody' || d.kind === 'partsGroup') {
        if (d.moved) {
          // the release runs the FULL alternating solve so joints end exactly
          // closed (the interactive passes were fast single-pass solves)
          if (d.kind === 'partMarker' && d.mated) solveAssemblyNow([]);
          scheduleSave();
          updateUI();
        } else {
          history.pop();
          updateHistoryButtons();
        }
        ui.drag = null;
        render();
        return;
      }
      // A drop onto another point only leaves a coincident constraint when Auto
      // dimension is ON — that is the whole point of the setting. This path used
      // to add it unconditionally, so dragging a point with auto dimension off
      // still produced "Coincident constraint added".
      if (settings.autoDimension !== 'off' &&
        d.moved && d.primary != null && d.snap && d.snap.id != null &&
        state.points[d.primary] && state.points[d.snap.id]) {
        if (!constraintExists(c => c.type === 'coincident' &&
          ((+c.p1 === d.primary && +c.p2 === d.snap.id) || (+c.p1 === d.snap.id && +c.p2 === d.primary)))) {
          const ck = newId();
          state.constraints[ck] = { id: ck, type: 'coincident', p1: d.primary, p2: d.snap.id };
          toast('Coincident constraint added');
        }
      }
      if (d.moved && (d.kind === 'point' || d.kind === 'entity' || d.kind === 'radius')) {
        requestSolve();
        invalidateLoops();
        updateUI();
      }
      if (!ui.drag.moved) {
        history.pop();
        updateHistoryButtons();
        updateUI();
      } else {
        scheduleSave();
        updateUI();
      }
      ui.drag = null;
      render();
    }
  }

  function onWheel(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    if (ui.offsetJob) {
      const step = ev.shiftKey ? 0.1 : (ev.altKey ? 5 : 1);
      const dir = ev.deltaY < 0 ? 1 : -1;
      ui.offsetJob.dist = Math.round((ui.offsetJob.dist + dir * step) * 100) / 100;
      refreshOffsetPreview();
      const hintEl = document.getElementById('status-hint');
      if (hintEl) hintEl.textContent = offsetHintText();
      render();
      return;
    }
    if (ui.tool === 'box' && ui.draft && ui.draft.type === 'box') {
      const pos = pointerPos(ev);
      const w = s2w(pos.x, pos.y);

    }

#btn-sidebar { color: var(--ink); }

/* Tablet / touch layout: the canvas must win the screen. The right sidebar
   is hidden by default and slides over (header hamburger toggles it). */
@media (max-width: 1100px), (pointer: coarse) and (max-width: 1400px) {
  #btn-sidebar { display: inline-flex; }
  #sidebar {
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    right: 0;
    bottom: 0;
    width: min(340px, 88vw);
    z-index: 60;
    transform: translateX(105%);
    transition: transform 0.22s ease;
    box-shadow: -12px 0 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.4);
    overflow-y: auto;
    max-height: none;
  }
  body.sidebar-open #sidebar { transform: translateX(0); }
  /* ribbon: icons only, larger touch targets; L/T/W/H wraps to its own
     centred line instead of being pushed off-screen */
  .ribbon-body { flex-wrap: wrap; row-gap: 4px; }
  .stage-left { left: 6px; }
  .stage-lth { top: 6px; }
  .tf input { width: 46px; }
  .rb-btn span { display: none; }
  .rb-btn { min-width: 52px; height: 48px; padding: 0 4px; justify-content: center; }
  .rb-btn svg { width: 26px; height: 26px; }
  .rb-mini { min-width: 44px; height: 44px; }
  .rb-mini svg { width: 24px; height: 24px; }
  .rb-caption { display: none; }
  .rb-center-spacer { display: none; }
  .stage-layers { width: 120px; padding: 5px 7px 6px; }
}
@media (max-width: 700px) {
  .qb-sep { display: none; }                    /* save every pixel of width */
  .quickbar { border-radius: 8px; }
}

#canvas {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: block;
  touch-action: none;
  cursor: default;
}

.sidebar {
  width: 268px;
  min-width: 200px;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  padding: 8px 10px 12px 2px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 8px;
}

.panel {
  background: var(--panel);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius);
  padding: 11px 12px;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow);
}

.panel h3 {
  margin: 0 0 9px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;

  function onDoubleClick(ev) {
    if (xfBusy() || ui.offsetJob) return;
    const pos = pointerPos(ev);
    // A mate job owns every click — its second pick IS a double click on the
    // same marker, and must never fall through to the part rename prompt.
    if (mateJob) return;
    // A double click on a badge is swallowed: it used to delete the constraint,
    // and letting it fall through would now split the line underneath instead.
    for (const z of ui.badgeZones || []) {
      if (pos.x >= z.x - 2 && pos.x <= z.x + z.w + 2 && pos.y >= z.y - 2 && pos.y <= z.y + z.h + 2) {
        return;
      }
    }
    const id = hitDimension(pos.x, pos.y);
    if (id != null) {
      editDimensionConstraint(id);
      return;
    }
    if (ui.draft && ui.draft.type === 'curve') {
      finishCurve(false);
      render();
      return;
    }
    const eh = hitEntity(pos.x, pos.y);
    if (eh != null) {
      const ent = state.entities[eh];
      // A part's name is editable in place: double click asks for the new name.
      if (ent && ent.type === 'part') {
        const newName = prompt('Part name', ent.name || ('Part ' + ent.id));
        if (newName != null) {
          pushHistory();
          ent.name = newName.trim();
          commit();
          updateUI();
        }
        render();
        return;
      }
      if (ent && ent.type === 'text') {
        ui.selection = [{ kind: 'entity', id: eh }];
        updateUI();
        render();
        openTextEditor(eh);
        return;
      }
      if (ent && ent.type === 'note') {
        ui.selection = [{ kind: 'entity', id: eh }];
        updateUI();
        render();
        editNote(ent);
        return;
      }
      if (ent && ent.type === 'array') {
        ui.selection = [{ kind: 'entity', id: eh }];
        updateUI();
        render();
        openArrayDialog(eh, ent.masters);
        return;
      }
      // a generated group remembers its generator and parameters
      if (ent && ent.gen && ent.type === 'art') {
        ui.selection = [{ kind: 'entity', id: eh }];
        updateUI();
        render();
        openGeneratorDialog(ent.gen);
        return;
      }
      insertPointOnEntity(eh, s2w(pos.x, pos.y), { addOnly: ev.shiftKey });
    }
  }

    const c = state.constraints[id];
    if (!isDimensionConstraint(c)) return;
    pushHistory();
    if (c.reference) {

        (function () {
          var t = 'dark';
          try {
            var mp = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('theme');
            if (mp === 'light' || mp === 'dark') {
              t = mp;
            } else {
              var saved = localStorage.getItem('minisketch.theme');
              if (saved === 'light' || saved === 'dark') t = saved;
            }
          } catch (err) { /* storage blocked */ }

                    <button class="rb-btn tool-btn" data-tool="curve" title="Curve / B&eacute;zier pen (K)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 15c4-10 10 4 14-10"/><circle cx="3" cy="15" r="1.4"/><circle cx="17" cy="5" r="1.4"/></svg><span>Curve</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn tool-btn" data-tool="arc" title="Arc (A)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 15a12 12 0 0 1 12-12"/><circle cx="3" cy="15" r="1.5"/><circle cx="15" cy="3" r="1.5"/></svg><span>Arc</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn tool-btn" data-tool="circle" title="Circle (C)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10" cy="10" r="6.5"/><circle cx="10" cy="10" r="1.2"/></svg><span>Circle</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn tool-btn" data-tool="box" title="Box (B)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="4" y="5.5" width="12" height="9" rx="0.5"/><circle cx="4" cy="5.5" r="1.4"/><circle cx="16" cy="14.5" r="1.4"/></svg><span>Box</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn tool-btn" data-tool="poly" title="Polygon / Star (Y)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 3l6 3.5v7L10 17l-6-3.5v-7z"/><circle cx="10" cy="10" r="1.2"/></svg><span>Poly</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn tool-btn" data-tool="text" title="Text (W)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 5h12M10 5v11M7.5 16h5"/></svg><span>Text</span></button>
                  </div>
                  <div class="rb-panel">
                    <button class="rb-btn tool-btn" data-tool="trim" title="Trim (I)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 4l9 9M16 4l-7.5 7.5"/><circle cx="4.5" cy="15" r="2"/><circle cx="15.5" cy="15" r="2"/></svg><span>Trim</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn tool-btn" data-tool="separate" title="Separate (E)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 13l5-5M12 16l5-5"/><circle cx="9" cy="7" r="2.4"/><circle cx="16.5" cy="4.5" r="0.4"/></svg><span>Separate</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" id="qb-note" title="Comment (;) &mdash; a yellow note, never machined"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 4h14v9H9l-4 4v-4H3z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Note</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn qb-act" id="qb-dimension" title="Dimension (D)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 14h14M3 12v4M17 12v4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M5 10l3-6h4l3 6z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Dim</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn qb-act" id="qb-refdim" title="Reference dimension (Shift+D)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 14h14M3 12v4M17 12v4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M6 8l3-5h2l3 5z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-dasharray="2 1.5"/></svg><span>Ref&nbsp;dim</span></button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="ribbon-page hidden" data-rpage="edit">
                  <div class="rb-panel">
                    <button class="rb-btn qb-act" id="qb-transform" title="Transform (R)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="4" y="4" width="12" height="12" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-dasharray="2.5 2"/><rect x="2.6" y="2.6" width="2.8" height="2.8" fill="currentColor"/><rect x="14.6" y="14.6" width="2.8" height="2.8" fill="currentColor"/></svg><span>Transform</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn qb-act" id="qb-offset" title="Offset (Ctrl+O)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="3" width="10" height="10" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><rect x="7" y="7" width="10" height="10" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-dasharray="2.5 2"/></svg><span>Offset</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn qb-act" id="qb-move" title="Move (Ctrl+M)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 2v16M2 10h16M10 2 7.5 4.5M10 2l2.5 2.5M10 18l-2.5-2.5M10 18l2.5-2.5M2 10l2.5-2.5M2 10l2.5 2.5M18 10l-2.5-2.5M18 10l-2.5 2.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Move</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn qb-act" id="qb-array" title="Array: grid or circular clones" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="2.5" y="2.5" width="6" height="6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><rect x="11.5" y="2.5" width="6" height="6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><rect x="2.5" y="11.5" width="6" height="6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><rect x="11.5" y="11.5" width="6" height="6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-dasharray="2 1.6"/></svg><span>Array</span></button>
                  </div>
                  <div class="rb-panel">
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-quick="flip-x" title="Flip horizontally" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 3v18" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-dasharray="2.4 2.2"/><path d="M9 7 4 12l5 5V7z" fill="currentColor"/></svg><span>Flip&nbsp;X</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-quick="flip-y" title="Flip vertically" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 12h18" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-dasharray="2.4 2.2"/><path d="M7 9l5-5 5 5H7z" fill="currentColor"/></svg><span>Flip&nbsp;Y</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-quick="rot-ccw" title="Rotate 90&deg; CCW" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8.5 5.5A7 7 0 1 1 5 12" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7"/><path d="M9.5 2.5 5.6 6l3.4 3.9z" fill="currentColor"/></svg><span>Rot&nbsp;&larr;</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-quick="rot-cw" title="Rotate 90&deg; CW" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M15.5 5.5A7 7 0 1 0 19 12" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7"/><path d="M14.5 2.5l3.9 3.5-3.4 3.9z" fill="currentColor"/></svg><span>Rot&nbsp;&rarr;</span></button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="ribbon-page hidden" data-rpage="boolean">
                  <div class="rb-panel">
                    <button class="rb-btn qb-act" data-quick="union" title="Boolean union (Ctrl++)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="9.5" cy="12" r="5.5" fill="currentColor" opacity="0.85"/><circle cx="14.5" cy="12" r="5.5" fill="currentColor"/></svg><span>Union</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-quick="cut" title="Boolean cut (Ctrl+-)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="9.5" cy="12" r="5.5" fill="currentColor"/><circle cx="14.5" cy="12" r="5.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" stroke-dasharray="2.6 2"/></svg><span>Cut</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-quick="intersect" title="Boolean intersect (Ctrl+0)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="9.5" cy="12" r="5.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" opacity="0.6"/><circle cx="14.5" cy="12" r="5.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" opacity="0.6"/><path d="M12 8.2a5.5 5.5 0 0 1 0 7.6 5.5 5.5 0 0 1 0-7.6z" fill="currentColor"/></svg><span>Intersect</span></button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="ribbon-page hidden" data-rpage="constrain">
                  <div class="rb-panel">
                    <button class="rb-btn qb-act" id="qb-fillet" title="Fillet (F)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 17V8a6 6 0 0 1 6 6v3" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M3 3l4 4M17 17l-4-4" stroke="currentColor" opacity="0.5"/></svg><span>Fillet</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-cmd="coincident"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="8" cy="10" r="3" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Coincident</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-cmd="horizontal"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="4" cy="13" r="1.8"/><circle cx="16" cy="7" r="1.8"/><path d="M4 13h12" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Horizontal</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-cmd="vertical"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="7" cy="4" r="1.8"/><circle cx="13" cy="16" r="1.8"/><path d="M7 4v12" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Vertical</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-cmd="parallel"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 16 9 4M11 16l4-12" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Parallel</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-cmd="perpendicular"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 16 16 4M4 4l5 5" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Perp</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-cmd="equal"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 8h12M4 12h12" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Equal</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-cmd="anchor"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10" cy="10" r="2.5" fill="currentColor"/><path d="M10 3v3M10 14v3M3 10h3M14 10h3" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Anchor</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-cmd="midpoint"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="3" cy="15" r="1.5"/><circle cx="17" cy="5" r="1.5"/><rect x="8" y="8" width="4" height="4" fill="currentColor"/></svg><span>Midpoint</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" data-cmd="tangent"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M2 5l9 5" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Tangent</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn qb-act" id="qb-join" title="Join points (J)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="6" cy="14" r="2.2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><circle cx="14" cy="6" r="2.2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M7.5 12.5 12.5 7.5" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Join</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn qb-act" id="qb-sharp" title="Sharp / smooth point (\\)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 17c5 0 5-14 14-14" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><circle cx="8.6" cy="8.6" r="1.8" fill="currentColor"/></svg><span>Sharp</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn qb-act" id="qb-clean" title="Clean to full loops (Z)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 16l5-5M9 15l6-6M11 3l6 6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><circle cx="6" cy="14" r="2.2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Clean</span></button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="ribbon-page hidden" data-rpage="machine">
                  <div class="rb-panel">
                    <button class="rb-btn" id="qb-cnc" title="Send to KaryaCNC (Alt+Q)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 12v4h14v-4M10 3v10M6.5 9.5 10 13l3.5-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Send&nbsp;CNC</span></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn" id="qb-nest" title="Nesting: upload selection / download result"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="2" y="2" width="16" height="16" rx="1.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M5.5 13.5l3-7 3 5 3-3" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/></svg><span>Nest</span></button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
      
        <div class="workspace">
      
          <div class="stage" id="stage">
            <canvas id="canvas"></canvas>
      
            <div class="stage-lth tf-strip" id="tf-strip" title="Selection bounds &mdash; type a number or a formula like 100*2 or 150%">
              <label class="tf"><span>L</span><input id="tf-left" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" disabled></label>
              <label class="tf"><span>T</span><input id="tf-top" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" disabled></label>
              <label class="tf"><span>W</span><input id="tf-width" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" disabled></label>
              <label class="tf"><span>H</span><input id="tf-height" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" disabled></label>
              <label class="tf-lock" id="tf-lock-label" title="Lock width / height ratio" aria-label="Lock width / height ratio">
                <input type="checkbox" id="tf-lock" disabled>
                <svg class="lock-icon" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true">
                  <path d="M4.7 7.1V5.2a3.3 3.3 0 0 1 6.6 0v1.9" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.35" stroke-linecap="round"/>
                  <rect x="3.3" y="7.1" width="9.4" height="6.4" rx="1.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.35"/>
                </svg>
              </label>
            </div>
      
            <div class="stage-left">
            <div class="stage-edit" id="stage-edit">
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini" id="qb-undo" title="Undo (Ctrl+Z)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 4 3.5 8.5 8 13" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6"/><path d="M3.5 8.5H12a4.5 4.5 0 0 1 0 9h-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini" id="qb-redo" title="Redo (Ctrl+Shift+Z)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 4l4.5 4.5L12 13" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6"/><path d="M16.5 8.5H8a4.5 4.5 0 0 0 0 9h2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini" id="qb-fit" title="Fit view (F)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2 6V2h4M14 2h4v4M18 14v4h-4M6 18H2v-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6"/><rect x="6.5" y="6.5" width="7" height="7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-dasharray="2 1.5"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini" id="qb-selectall" title="Select all (Ctrl+A)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="3" width="10" height="10" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-dasharray="2.5 2"/><rect x="7" y="7" width="10" height="10" fill="currentColor" opacity="0.55"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini qb-act" id="qb-copy" title="Copy (Ctrl+C)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="7" y="7" width="9" height="9" rx="1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M13 7V5a1 1 0 0 0-1-1H5a1 1 0 0 0-1 1v7a1 1 0 0 0 1 1h2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini" id="qb-paste" title="Paste (Ctrl+V)"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="4" y="4" width="12" height="13" rx="1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M7 4V2.5h6V4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M7.5 10h5M7.5 13h5" stroke="currentColor"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini qb-act" id="qb-dup" title="Duplicate in place (Ctrl+D)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="2.5" y="6.5" width="9" height="9" rx="1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><rect x="8.5" y="4.5" width="9" height="9" rx="1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-dasharray="2 1.6"/></svg></button>
                    <button class="rb-btn rb-mini qb-act" id="qb-delete" title="Delete selection (Del)" disabled><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 6h10l-.8 11H5.8z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"/><path d="M3.5 6h13M8 4h4M8.5 9v5M11.5 9v5" stroke="currentColor"/></svg></button>
                    <div class="tf-toggle-wrapper" style="display:flex;gap:4px;margin-top:8px">
                      <button class="rb-btn rb-mini toggle shift-toggle" data-toggle="shift" title="Shift (toggle)">Shift</button>
                      <button class="rb-btn rb-mini toggle ctrl-toggle" data-toggle="ctrl" title="Ctrl (toggle)">Ctrl</button>
                      <button class="rb-btn rb-mini toggle alt-toggle" data-toggle="alt" title="Alt (toggle)">Alt</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            <div class="stage-layers" id="stage-layers" title="Layers &mdash; the active layer receives new geometry">
              <div class="stage-layers-head">Layers</div>
              <div id="layers" class="layers"></div>
              <div class="layer-tools">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-add-layer" title="Add a new layer and make it active">+</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-layer-up" title="Move the highlighted layer up (draws on top)">&#8593;</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-layer-down" title="Move the highlighted layer down (draws below)">&#8595;</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-dim-layers" title="Dim other layers: fade every layer but the active one">&#9680;</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            </div>
      
          
            <div class="stage-colors" id="stage-colors" title="Garis = warna operasi KaryaCNC; kotak = loop fill">
              <div class="swatches" id="swatches"></div>
              <div class="stage-colors-sep" aria-hidden="true"></div>
              <div class="stage-fills" id="loop-fill-row">
                <button class="swatch fill-sq fill-none" data-fill="none" title="No loop fill" aria-label="No loop fill"></button>
                <button class="swatch fill-sq fill-gray" data-fill="gray" title="Gray &mdash; material boundary: everything inside gets flipped" aria-label="Loop fill gray"></button>
                <button class="swatch fill-sq fill-yellow" data-fill="yellow" title="Yellow &mdash; flip the offset direction of this contour" aria-label="Loop fill yellow"></button>
              </div>
            </div>
        <section class="xf-float" id="transform-panel">
          <h3>Transform <span class="count">R</span></h3>
          <div class="xf-fields">
            <label class="xf-field" title="Angle in degrees"><span class="ang" aria-label="Angle">&ang;</span><input id="xf-angle" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" disabled></label>
            <label class="xf-field" title="Scale X"><span>X</span><input id="xf-sx" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" disabled></label>
            <label class="xf-field" title="Scale Y"><span>Y</span><input id="xf-sy" type="text" inputmode="decimal" spellcheck="false" disabled></label>
            <label class="tf-lock" id="xf-lock-label" title="Lock width / height ratio" aria-label="Lock width / height ratio">
              <input type="checkbox" id="xf-lock" disabled>
              <svg class="lock-icon" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true">
                <path d="M4.7 7.1V5.2a3.3 3.3 0 0 1 6.6 0v1.9" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.35" stroke-linecap="round"/>
                <rect x="3.3" y="7.1" width="9.4" height="6.4" rx="1.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.35"/>
              </svg>
            </label>
          </div>
          <div class="row">
            <button class="btn small primary" id="xf-apply" disabled>Apply</button>
            <button class="btn small" id="xf-cancel" disabled>Cancel</button>
            <button class="btn small" id="xf-center" title="Move the pivot to the centre of the selection">Center pivot</button>
          </div>
        </section>
          </div>
      
          <div class="vsplit" id="vsplit" role="separator" aria-orientation="vertical"
               aria-label="Resize panels" tabindex="0"></div>
      
          <aside class="sidebar" id="sidebar">
            <section class="panel" id="chat-panel">
              <h3>AI Chat <span class="count" id="chat-count">0</span></h3>
              <div class="row tab-bar" style="margin-bottom:6px">
                <button class="btn small is-on" data-chat-tab="chat">Chat</button>
                <button class="btn small" data-chat-tab="log">Log</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="chat-new" title="Bersihkan percakapan dan mulai chat baru (geometri di worksheet tidak disentuh)">New</button>
              </div>
              <ul class="list" id="chat-log"></ul>
              <div class="row" style="margin-top:6px">
                <input id="chat-input" type="text" placeholder='kotak 100x60 di 0,0…' style="flex:1 1 auto;min-width:0;font:inherit;font-size:11.5px;padding:4px 7px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false">
                <button class="btn small danger hidden" id="chat-stop" title="Hentikan permintaan yang sedang berjalan">■ Stop</button>
                <button class="btn small primary" id="chat-send">Kirim</button>
              </div>
            </section>
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Selection</h3>
              <div id="selection-info" class="selection-info">Nothing selected</div>
              <div class="row">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-name" title="Name the selection (Ctrl+N). A name is only a label: the geometry is not touched; leave it empty to clear">Name</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-to-curve" title="Convert the selected circle or arc to a Bézier curve, or a text object into chained editable lines (one line per outline segment)" disabled>&rarr; Curve</button>
                <button class="btn small toggle" id="btn-group-select" aria-pressed="false" title="Group select: a click takes the whole group. Alt+click always does the opposite.">Group <i class="key">Alt</i></button>
              </div>
              <div class="row">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-group" title="Group the selection into one object (Ctrl+G)">Group</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-ungroup" title="Ungroup: explode grouped/pasted objects back to editable curves (Ctrl+Shift+G)">Ungroup</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-connected" title="Select connected: extend the selection to every shape chained to it (N)">Connected</button>
              </div>
              <div class="constraint-block hidden" id="constraint-block">
                <div class="constraint-head">Constraints <span class="count" id="constraint-count">0</span></div>
                <ul class="list" id="constraint-list"></ul>
              </div>
            </section>
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Parts <span class="count" id="parts-count">0</span></h3>
              <div class="row" style="margin-bottom:6px">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-make-part" title="Bake the selection into a rigid part: one polyline per contour plus a marker on every node and segment midpoint. The master geometry is frozen &mdash; a part only rotates and translates, and dragging one of its markers moves the whole part">Make part</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-mate" title="Mate: click a marker on one part, then a marker on another part (pin &mdash; rotate about the point) or an edge midpoint (slider &mdash; rotate and slide along the line). Clicking the same marker twice grounds that part (Alt+T)">Mate <i class="key">Alt+T</i></button>
              </div>
              <ul class="list" id="parts-list"></ul>
            </section>
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Tools</h3>
              <div class="row" id="tools-row">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-array" title="Clone array: grid or circular copies of the selection. Double click the array to edit its parameters or refresh the clones">Array</button>
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-nest" title="Nesting: with objects selected (plus a 'nest' text/comment) it uploads them to the KaryaCNC nesting server; with only the 'nest' marker — or nothing — selected it downloads the result and asks you to click where to place it">Nest</button>
              </div>
              <div class="constraint-head">Arrays <span class="count" id="array-count">0</span></div>
              <ul class="list" id="array-list"></ul>
            </section>
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Named selections</h3>
              <div class="row" style="margin-bottom:6px">
                <input id="nsel-name" type="text" placeholder="selection name" style="flex:1 1 auto;min-width:0;font:inherit;font-size:11.5px;padding:4px 7px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px">
                <button class="btn small" id="nsel-save">Save</button>
              </div>
              <ul class="list" id="nsel-list"></ul>
            </section>
      
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Constrain</h3>
              <div class="constraint-grid">
                <button class="btn small" id="btn-dimension">Dimension <i class="key">D</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="coincident">Coincident <i class="key">O</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="horizontal">Horizontal <i class="key">H</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="vertical">Vertical <i class="key">V</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="parallel">Parallel <i class="key">P</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="perpendicular">Perp <i class="key">[</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="equal">Equal <i class="key">Q</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="anchor">Anchor <i class="key">.</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="midpoint">Midpoint <i class="key">M</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="tangent">Tangent <i class="key">T</i></button>
                <button class="btn small" data-cmd="clear">Clear all</button>
              </div>
                    </section>
      
            <section class="panel">
              <h3>Closed loops <span class="count" id="loop-count">0</span></h3>
              <button class="btn small" id="btn-clean-loops" title="Weld doubled points around every loop: two points that sit within the join tolerance become one point, so each loop is really closed. Works loop by loop, so it stays fast on a big drawing">Clean up loop</button>
              <ul class="list" id="loop-list"></ul>
            </section>

          <div class="cnc-status">Double-click the text any time to edit. Dimension the green handles: one dimension keeps the glyph ratio, two release it. <b>Detail</b> is the outline accuracy in mm (0.08 default; larger = fewer points). Send traces the glyphs automatically. With <b>Fit real curves</b> on, &#8594; Curve rebuilds the glyphs as a few real Bezier curves; with it off you get one straight line per contour segment instead. A <b>Single-line</b> font is a stroke font: &rarr; Curve and Send produce open paths, one per stem — what engraving / V-carving wants.</div>
          <div class="modal-actions">
            <button class="btn small" id="text-curve" title="Replace the text object with editable geometry — closed curves per glyph outline, or open splines per stroke for a Single-line font">&#8594; Curve</button>

  function onPointerUp(ev) {
    // the gesture is over: the solve below is allowed again
    dragSolveSuppressed = false;
    cancelDragAutoSolve();
    if (ui.tool === 'curve' && ui.draft && ui.draft.drag) {
      ui.draft.drag = null;
      render();
      return;
    }
    if (ui.drag && ui.drag.kind && ui.drag.kind.indexOf('xf') === 0) {
      const d = ui.drag;
      ui.drag = null;
      const p = ui.transform.pending;
      if (p && d.kind !== 'xfPivot' && !p.moved) xfDiscardEmpty();
      updateCanvasCursor();
      xfUpdatePanel();
      render();
      return;
    }
    if (ui.drag && ui.drag.kind === 'rubber') {
      const d = ui.drag;
      ui.drag = null;
      const p = pointerPos(ev);
      const moved = Math.abs(p.x - d.start.x) > 3 || Math.abs(p.y - d.start.y) > 3;
      if (d.deferredLoop && !moved) {
        const m = d.deferredMods || {};
        applyLoopClick(d.deferredLoop, !!m.add, !!m.sub, !!m.alt);
        return;
      }
      applyRubberSelection(d, p);
      return;
    }
    if (ui.pan) {
      ui.pan = null;
      updateCanvasCursor();
      scheduleViewSave();
      render();
      return;
    }
    if (ui.drag) {
      const d = ui.drag;
      // A part-marker drag moved pose data, not points: no constraint solve,
      // no loop invalidation beyond the pose footprint change. The release
      // runs the FULL alternating solve (the interactive passes were fast).
      if (d.kind === 'partMarker' || d.kind === 'partBody' || d.kind === 'partsGroup') {
        if (d.moved) {
          // the release runs the FULL alternating solve so joints end exactly
          // closed (the interactive passes were fast single-pass solves)
          if (d.kind === 'partMarker' && d.mated) solveAssemblyNow([]);
          scheduleSave();
          updateUI();
        } else {
          history.pop();
          updateHistoryButtons();
        }
        ui.drag = null;
        render();
        return;
      }
      // A drop onto another point only leaves a coincident constraint when Auto
      // dimension is ON — that is the whole point of the setting. This path used
      // to add it unconditionally, so dragging a point with auto dimension off
      // still produced "Coincident constraint added".
      if (settings.autoDimension !== 'off' &&
        d.moved && d.primary != null && d.snap && d.snap.id != null &&
        state.points[d.primary] && state.points[d.snap.id]) {
        if (!constraintExists(c => c.type === 'coincident' &&
          ((+c.p1 === d.primary && +c.p2 === d.snap.id) || (+c.p1 === d.snap.id && +c.p2 === d.primary)))) {
          const ck = newId();
          state.constraints[ck] = { id: ck, type: 'coincident', p1: d.primary, p2: d.snap.id };
          toast('Coincident constraint added');
        }
      }
      if (d.moved && (d.kind === 'point' || d.kind === 'entity' || d.kind === 'radius')) {
        requestSolve();
        invalidateLoops();
        updateUI();
      }
      if (!ui.drag.moved) {
        history.pop();
        updateHistoryButtons();
        updateUI();
      } else {
        scheduleSave();
        updateUI();
      }
      ui.drag = null;
      render();

      // cross-layer: it reaches into every visible, unlocked layer.
      ui.drag = { kind: 'rubber', start: pos, additive: addMod || subMod, subtract: ev.altKey, crossLayer: !!(ev.ctrlKey && ev.shiftKey) };
      return;

> _Reasoning_
> All changes have been made. Let me provide a summary of what was done.

The touch/pointer UI fixes for tablet/phone have been implemented. Here's a summary of the changes:

## Changes Made

### 1. `app.js` - Modifier toggle state integration

- **onPointerDown** (line ~16320): Added `ui.ctrlDown = ev.ctrlKey || ui.ctrlToggle;` so Ctrl modifier state works with touch toggles. Also added `ui.ctrlDown` tracking alongside existing `shiftDown` and `altDown`.

- **onPointerMove** (line ~17558): Added `ui.ctrlDown = ev.ctrlKey;` to track Ctrl state during drag moves.

- **Rubber band selection** (line ~16611, ~16621): Updated `subtract: ev.altKey || ui.altToggle` so Alt key behavior works with the touch toggle button. Both the deferred loop and regular rubber selection now respect the Alt toggle.

- **onWheel** (line ~18427-18445): Updated wheel step calculations to use toggle state:
  - `ui.offsetJob` step: Uses `(ev.shiftKey || ui.shiftToggle)` and `(ev.altKey || ui.altToggle)` 
  - Box drafting fillet step: Uses `(ev.shiftKey || ui.shiftToggle)`
  - Poly drafting inner step: Uses `ev.shiftKey || ui.shiftToggle`

### 2. `index.html` - Toggle buttons already present

The Shift, Ctrl, and Alt toggle buttons were already added to the stage-left area (lines 198-200):
- `<button class="rb-btn rb-mini toggle shift-toggle" data-toggle="shift" title="Shift (toggle)">Shift</button>`
- `<button class="rb-btn rb-mini toggle ctrl-toggle" data-toggle="ctrl" title="Ctrl (toggle)">Ctrl</button>`
- `<button class="rb-btn rb-mini toggle alt-toggle" data-toggle="alt" title="Alt (toggle)">Alt</button>`

### 3. `styles.css` - Toggle styling already present

The `.tf-toggle-wrapper .toggle` CSS class already provides styling for the toggle buttons with active state styling.

## How it works

When no modifier key is physically pressed on touch devices:
- The touch toggle buttons (Shift/Ctrl/Alt) can be tapped to simulate those modifiers
- The UI state (`ui.shiftToggle`, `ui.ctrlToggle`, `ui.altToggle`) tracks which toggles are active
- All the existing modifier logic (`ev.shiftKey`, `ev.ctrlKey`, `ev.altKey`) now falls back to the toggle state when the key is not pressed

This enables tablet/phone users to use Shift, Ctrl, and Alt modifiers for selection, zoom/pan, and other operations through the on-screen toggle buttons.
